  <!-- FIREBASE: AUTENTICACIÓN + FIRESTORE -->
  <script type="module">
    import { initializeApp } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/12.18.0/firebase-app.js";
    import {
      getAuth,
      createUserWithEmailAndPassword,
      signInWithEmailAndPassword,
      updateProfile,
      signOut,
      onAuthStateChanged
    } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/12.18.0/firebase-auth.js";
    import {
      getFirestore,
      doc,
      getDoc,
      setDoc
    } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/12.18.0/firebase-firestore.js";
    const firebaseConfig = {
      apiKey: "AIzaSyAwHW2No3L9liaJWXRlH4X0WW7gauVHNLA",
      authDomain: "planner-fi.firebaseapp.com",
      projectId: "planner-fi",
      storageBucket: "planner-fi.firebasestorage.app",
      messagingSenderId: "330658275949",
      appId: "1:330658275949:web:8da27b562df820d7c85564"
    };

    const app = initializeApp(firebaseConfig);
    const auth = getAuth(app);
    const db = getFirestore(app);

    window.plannerFiFirebaseAuth = auth;
    window.plannerFiFirestore = db;
    window.plannerFiFirestoreDoc = doc;
    window.plannerFiFirestoreGetDoc = getDoc;
    window.plannerFiFirestoreSetDoc = setDoc;

    window.firebaseCreateAccount = async (name, email, password) => {
      const credential = await createUserWithEmailAndPassword(auth, email, password);
      if (name && credential.user) {
        await updateProfile(credential.user, { displayName: name });
      }
      return credential.user;
    };

    window.firebaseLogin = async (email, password) => {
      const credential = await signInWithEmailAndPassword(auth, email, password);
      return credential.user;
    };

    window.firebaseLogout = () => signOut(auth);

    window.firebaseAuthReady = new Promise((resolve) => {
      onAuthStateChanged(auth, (user) => {
        window.plannerFiFirebaseUser = user || null;
        window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('plannerfi-auth-state', { detail: user || null }));
        resolve(user || null);
      });
    });
  </script>

  <!-- JavaScript Application Logic -->
  <script>
    // State Management & Independent Profiles
    let currentProfile = 'Personal';
    let userConfig = null;
    let transactions = [];
    let debts = [];
    let savedModels = [];
    let savedModelFiles = [];
    let familyMembers = [];
    
    // NUEVAS VARIABLES PARA EL MÓDULO TRABAJO Y SUELDO (independientes por perfil en localStorage)
    let workSettings = {};
    let workDays = [];
    let salaryReceipts = [];
    let workNotes = [];

    let pieChartInstance = null;
    let barChartInstance = null;
    let workHoursPieChartInstance = null;
    let salaryEvolutionChartInstance = null;

    // Initialize App
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
      lucide.createIcons();
      checkUserRegistration();
      const receiptInput = document.getElementById('debtReceiptFile');
      if (receiptInput) receiptInput.addEventListener('change', () => {
        const f=receiptInput.files[0], box=document.getElementById('debtReceiptPreview');
        if (!f) { box.classList.add('hidden'); return; }
        box.classList.remove('hidden');
        box.innerHTML=`<b>Archivo seleccionado:</b> ${escapeHtml(f.name)}<br><span class="text-slate-400">${Math.round(f.size/1024)} KB · ${escapeHtml(f.type || 'tipo desconocido')}</span>`;
      });
    });

    let pfPresentationIndex = 0;
    const pfPresentationTotal = 7;

    function pfRenderPresentation(index = 0) {
      const slides = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#plannerfiLanding .pf-slide'));
      if (!slides.length) return;
      pfPresentationIndex = Math.max(0, Math.min(index, slides.length - 1));
      slides.forEach((slide, i) => slide.classList.toggle('active', i === pfPresentationIndex));
      const count = document.getElementById('pfPresentCount');
      if (count) count.textContent = `${pfPresentationIndex + 1} de ${slides.length}`;
      const dots = document.getElementById('pfPresentationDots');
      if (dots) {
        dots.innerHTML = slides.map((_, i) => `<button type="button" class="pf-dot ${i===pfPresentationIndex?'active':''}" aria-label="Ir a la sección ${i+1}" onclick="pfPresentationGo(${i})"></button>`).join('');
      }
      const prev = document.getElementById('pfPrevBtn');
      if (prev) prev.disabled = pfPresentationIndex === 0;
      const next = document.getElementById('pfNextBtn');
      if (next) next.innerHTML = pfPresentationIndex === slides.length - 1 ? 'Finalizar <i data-lucide="log-in" class="w-4 h-4"></i>' : 'Seguir <i data-lucide="arrow-right" class="w-4 h-4"></i>';
      setTimeout(() => lucide.createIcons(), 0);
    }

    function pfPresentationNext() {
      if (pfPresentationIndex >= pfPresentationTotal - 1) return enterPlannerFi();
      pfRenderPresentation(pfPresentationIndex + 1);
    }
    function pfPresentationPrev() { pfRenderPresentation(pfPresentationIndex - 1); }
    function pfPresentationGo(index) { pfRenderPresentation(index); }

    function showPlannerLanding() {
      document.body.classList.remove('pf-app-open');
      pfRenderPresentation(0);
      window.scrollTo({top:0, behavior:'instant'});
      setTimeout(() => lucide.createIcons(), 0);
    }

    let firebaseAuthMode = 'login';

    function setAuthMode(mode) {
      firebaseAuthMode = mode === 'register' ? 'register' : 'login';
      const title = document.getElementById('authModalTitle');
      const subtitle = document.getElementById('authModalSubtitle');
      const submit = document.getElementById('firebaseAuthSubmit');
      const nameField = document.getElementById('authNameField');
      const nameInput = document.getElementById('loginNameInput');
      const loginTab = document.getElementById('authLoginTab');
      const registerTab = document.getElementById('authRegisterTab');
      const password = document.getElementById('loginPasswordInput');
      const error = document.getElementById('firebaseAuthError');
      if (error) { error.textContent = ''; error.classList.add('hidden'); }
      if (firebaseAuthMode === 'register') {
        title.textContent = 'Crear Cuenta';
        subtitle.textContent = 'Registrate con tu nombre, correo y contraseña.';
        submit.textContent = 'Crear Cuenta';
        nameField.classList.remove('hidden');
        nameInput.required = true;
        password.autocomplete = 'new-password';
        loginTab.className = 'flex-1 py-2 rounded-lg text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-300 text-sm font-bold';
        registerTab.className = 'flex-1 py-2 rounded-lg bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 shadow-sm text-sm font-bold';
      } else {
        title.textContent = 'Iniciar Sesión';
        subtitle.textContent = 'Ingresá con tu correo y contraseña para acceder a PLANNER.FI.';
        submit.textContent = 'Iniciar Sesión';
        nameField.classList.add('hidden');
        nameInput.required = false;
        password.autocomplete = 'current-password';
        loginTab.className = 'flex-1 py-2 rounded-lg bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 shadow-sm text-sm font-bold';
        registerTab.className = 'flex-1 py-2 rounded-lg text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-300 text-sm font-bold';
      }
    }

    function firebaseErrorMessage(error) {
      const code = error?.code || '';
      const messages = {
        'auth/email-already-in-use': 'Ese correo ya está registrado. Iniciá sesión.',
        'auth/invalid-email': 'El correo electrónico no es válido.',
        'auth/weak-password': 'La contraseña debe tener al menos 6 caracteres.',
        'auth/invalid-credential': 'Correo o contraseña incorrectos.',
        'auth/user-not-found': 'No existe una cuenta con ese correo.',
        'auth/wrong-password': 'La contraseña es incorrecta.',
        'auth/too-many-requests': 'Demasiados intentos. Esperá unos minutos y probá nuevamente.',
        'auth/network-request-failed': 'No se pudo conectar con Firebase. Revisá tu conexión a Internet.',
        'auth/configuration-not-found': 'Firebase Authentication no está configurado para este proyecto. Activá Email/Password en Firebase Console.',
        'auth/operation-not-allowed': 'El método Email/Password no está habilitado en Firebase Authentication.'
      };
      return messages[code] || ('No se pudo completar la autenticación. ' + (error?.message || 'Revisá los datos e intentá nuevamente.'));
    }

    function showFirebaseAuthError(message) {
      const box = document.getElementById('firebaseAuthError');
      if (!box) return;
      box.textContent = message;
      box.classList.remove('hidden');
    }

    function saveFirebaseUserToLocalProfile(firebaseUser, typedName = '') {
      const name = typedName || firebaseUser?.displayName || (firebaseUser?.email || '').split('@')[0] || 'Usuario';
      userConfig = {
        name,
        email: firebaseUser?.email || '',
        uid: firebaseUser?.uid || '',
        birthday: '',
        workStatus: '',
        language: 'es',
        country: 'AR',
        currency: 'ARS',
        photo: firebaseUser?.photoURL || ''
      };
      return userConfig;
    }

    async function pfGetFirebaseUser() {
      const user = window.plannerFiFirebaseUser || await window.firebaseAuthReady;
      if (!user?.uid) throw new Error('No hay una sesión de Firebase activa.');
      return user;
    }

    function pfUserDoc(uid, section) {
      return window.plannerFiFirestoreDoc(window.plannerFiFirestore, 'users', uid, 'appData', section);
    }

    async function pfReadSection(uid, section) {
      const snap = await window.plannerFiFirestoreGetDoc(pfUserDoc(uid, section));
      return snap.exists() ? (snap.data() || {}) : null;
    }

    async function pfWriteSection(uid, section, data) {
      await window.plannerFiFirestoreSetDoc(pfUserDoc(uid, section), {
        ...data,
        updatedAt: new Date().toISOString()
      }, { merge: true });
    }

    async function pfSaveUserProfile() {
      const firebaseUser = await pfGetFirebaseUser();
      if (!userConfig) saveFirebaseUserToLocalProfile(firebaseUser);
      await pfWriteSection(firebaseUser.uid, 'profile', {
        name: userConfig.name || firebaseUser.displayName || 'Usuario',
        email: firebaseUser.email || userConfig.email || '',
        uid: firebaseUser.uid,
        birthday: userConfig.birthday || '',
        workStatus: userConfig.workStatus || '',
        language: userConfig.language || 'es',
        country: userConfig.country || 'AR',
        currency: userConfig.currency || 'ARS',
        photo: userConfig.photo || firebaseUser.photoURL || ''
      });
    }

    async function enterPlannerFi() {
      document.body.classList.add('pf-app-open');
      try {
        const firebaseUser = await window.firebaseAuthReady;
        if (!firebaseUser) {
          openModal('loginModal');
          setAuthMode('login');
        } else {
          saveFirebaseUserToLocalProfile(firebaseUser);
          await loadProfileData();
        }
      } catch (error) {
        openModal('loginModal');
        setAuthMode('login');
        showFirebaseAuthError(firebaseErrorMessage(error));
      }
      setTimeout(() => lucide.createIcons(), 0);
    }

    function checkUserRegistration() {
      showPlannerLanding();
      window.addEventListener('plannerfi-auth-state', (event) => {
        const firebaseUser = event.detail;
        window.plannerFiFirebaseUser = firebaseUser || null;
      });
    }

    async function handleFirebaseAuthSubmit(event) {
      event.preventDefault();
      const email = document.getElementById('loginEmailInput').value.trim();
      const password = document.getElementById('loginPasswordInput').value;
      const name = document.getElementById('loginNameInput').value.trim();
      const button = document.getElementById('firebaseAuthSubmit');
      if (!email || !password) return showFirebaseAuthError('Ingresá correo y contraseña.');
      if (firebaseAuthMode === 'register' && !name) return showFirebaseAuthError('Ingresá tu nombre.');
      if (firebaseAuthMode === 'register' && password.length < 6) return showFirebaseAuthError('La contraseña debe tener al menos 6 caracteres.');
      const oldText = button.textContent;
      button.disabled = true;
      button.textContent = firebaseAuthMode === 'register' ? 'Creando cuenta…' : 'Ingresando…';
      const error = document.getElementById('firebaseAuthError');
      error.classList.add('hidden');
      try {
        let firebaseUser;
        if (firebaseAuthMode === 'register') {
          firebaseUser = await window.firebaseCreateAccount(name, email, password);
        } else {
          firebaseUser = await window.firebaseLogin(email, password);
        }
        saveFirebaseUserToLocalProfile(firebaseUser, name);
        closeModal('loginModal');
        document.body.classList.add('pf-app-open');
        await loadProfileData();
      } catch (err) {
        showFirebaseAuthError(firebaseErrorMessage(err));
      } finally {
        button.disabled = false;
        button.textContent = oldText;
      }
    }

    async function handleFirebaseLogout() {
      if (!confirm('¿Querés cerrar sesión de PLANNER.FI?')) return;
      try {
        await window.firebaseLogout();
        userConfig = null;
        document.body.classList.remove('pf-app-open');
        showPlannerLanding();
      } catch (error) {
        alert(firebaseErrorMessage(error));
      }
    }

    // Compatibilidad con llamadas antiguas del código: ya no existe el código simulado 1234.
    function sendAuthCode() { setAuthMode('login'); }
    function verifyAuthCode() { document.getElementById('firebaseAuthForm')?.requestSubmit(); }

    async function loadProfileData() {
      const firebaseUser = await pfGetFirebaseUser();
      const financial = await pfReadSection(firebaseUser.uid, 'financial');
      const work = await pfReadSection(firebaseUser.uid, 'work');
      const profile = await pfReadSection(firebaseUser.uid, 'profile');

      // IMPORTANTE: la fuente de datos de la cuenta autenticada es Firestore.
      // No se leen los antiguos datos financieros de localStorage, evitando que
      // una cuenta nueva herede información de otra cuenta usada en el mismo navegador.
      transactions = Array.isArray(financial?.transactions) ? financial.transactions : [];
      debts = Array.isArray(financial?.debts) ? financial.debts : [];
      savedModels = Array.isArray(financial?.savedModels) ? financial.savedModels : [];
      savedModelFiles = Array.isArray(financial?.savedModelFiles) ? financial.savedModelFiles : [];
      familyMembers = Array.isArray(financial?.familyMembers) ? financial.familyMembers : [];

      workSettings = work?.workSettings || {
        companyName: '',
        jobTitle: '',
        payType: 'Mensual',
        baseSalary: 0,
        hourlyRate: 0,
        normalHoursPerDay: 8,
        overtime50Rate: 50,
        overtime100Rate: 100
      };
      workDays = Array.isArray(work?.workDays) ? work.workDays : [];
      salaryReceipts = Array.isArray(work?.salaryReceipts) ? work.salaryReceipts : [];
      workNotes = Array.isArray(work?.workNotes) ? work.workNotes : [];

      if (profile) {
        userConfig = {
          name: profile.name || firebaseUser.displayName || (firebaseUser.email || '').split('@')[0] || 'Usuario',
          email: firebaseUser.email || profile.email || '',
          uid: firebaseUser.uid,
          birthday: profile.birthday || '',
          workStatus: profile.workStatus || '',
          language: profile.language || 'es',
          country: profile.country || 'AR',
          currency: profile.currency || 'ARS',
          photo: profile.photo || firebaseUser.photoURL || ''
        };
      } else {
        saveFirebaseUserToLocalProfile(firebaseUser);
        pfSaveUserProfile();
      }

      updateUserProfileUI();
      updateAll();
    }

    function updateUserProfileUI() {
      if (!userConfig) return;
      document.getElementById('userNameDisplay').innerText = userConfig.name;
      document.getElementById('userEmailDisplay').innerText = userConfig.email;
      document.getElementById('bannerUserName').innerText = userConfig.name;
      const container = document.getElementById('userAvatarContainer');
      const tabAvatar = document.getElementById('profileTabAvatar');
      if (userConfig.photo) {
        container.innerHTML = `<img src="${userConfig.photo}" class="w-full h-full object-cover">`;
        tabAvatar.innerHTML = `<img src="${userConfig.photo}" class="w-full h-full object-cover">`;
      } else {
        const initials = userConfig.name.split(' ').map(n => n[0]).join('').substring(0, 2).toUpperCase();
        document.getElementById('userAvatarText').innerText = initials || 'US';
        document.getElementById('profileTabAvatarText').innerText = initials || 'US';
      }
      // Sync profile tab inputs
      document.getElementById('profileNameInput').value = userConfig.name;
      document.getElementById('profileEmailInput').value = userConfig.email;
      document.getElementById('profilePhotoInput').value = userConfig.photo || '';
      document.getElementById('profileBirthdayInput').value = userConfig.birthday || '';
      document.getElementById('profileWorkStatusInput').value = userConfig.workStatus || '';
      if (document.getElementById('profileLanguageInput')) document.getElementById('profileLanguageInput').value = 'es';
      document.getElementById('profileCountryInput').value = userConfig.country || 'AR';
      document.getElementById('profileCurrencyInput').value = userConfig.currency || countryCurrency(userConfig.country || 'AR');
      document.getElementById('profileTypeInput').value = currentProfile;
      
      // Sync work settings modal inputs
      if(document.getElementById('wsCompanyName')) {
        document.getElementById('wsCompanyName').value = workSettings.companyName || '';
        document.getElementById('wsJobTitle').value = workSettings.jobTitle || '';
        document.getElementById('wsPayType').value = workSettings.payType || 'Mensual';
        document.getElementById('wsBaseSalary').value = workSettings.baseSalary || 0;
        document.getElementById('wsHourlyRate').value = workSettings.hourlyRate || 0;
        document.getElementById('wsNormalHoursPerDay').value = workSettings.normalHoursPerDay || 8;
        document.getElementById('wsOvertime50Rate').value = workSettings.overtime50Rate || 50;
        document.getElementById('wsOvertime100Rate').value = workSettings.overtime100Rate || 100;
      }

      // Handle Family section view
      const famSection = document.getElementById('familyAccessSection');
      if (currentProfile === 'Familiar') {
        famSection.classList.remove('hidden');
        renderFamilyMembers();
      } else {
        famSection.classList.add('hidden');
      }
    }

    async function handleProfilePhotoSelect(event) {
      const file = event.target.files && event.target.files[0];
      if (!file) return;
      if (!file.type.startsWith('image/')) return alert('Seleccioná un archivo de imagen válido.');
      // Sin Firebase Storage: guardamos la foto como Data URL en el documento privado del usuario.
      if (file.size > 600 * 1024) return alert('La imagen es demasiado grande. Elegí una foto de hasta 600 KB.');
      try {
        const firebaseUser = await pfGetFirebaseUser();
        if (!firebaseUser?.uid) throw new Error('No hay una sesión autenticada.');
        const reader = new FileReader();
        reader.onload = async () => {
          try {
            userConfig = userConfig || {};
            userConfig.photo = reader.result;
            document.getElementById('profilePhotoInput').value = reader.result;
            await pfSaveUserProfile();
            updateUserProfileUI();
          } catch (error) {
            console.error(error);
            alert('No se pudo guardar la foto de perfil.');
          }
        };
        reader.onerror = () => alert('No se pudo leer la imagen seleccionada.');
        reader.readAsDataURL(file);
      } catch (error) {
        console.error(error);
        alert('No se pudo guardar la foto de perfil.');
      } finally {
        event.target.value = '';
      }
    }
    async function changeLanguage(lang) {
      const t = translations[lang] || translations.es;
      const map = {
        'nav-dashboard': t.dashboard, 'nav-transactions': t.transactions, 'nav-debts': t.debts,
        'nav-work': t.work, 'nav-education': t.education, 'nav-models': t.models, 'nav-profile': t.profile
      };
      Object.entries(map).forEach(([id, text]) => {
        const el = document.getElementById(id);
        if (el && el.querySelector('span')) el.querySelector('span').textContent = text;
      });
      const title = document.querySelector('title');
      if (title) title.textContent = 'PLANNER.FI - ' + (lang === 'en' ? 'Advanced Personal Finance Manager' : lang === 'pt' ? 'Gestor Financeiro Pessoal Avançado' : 'Gestor Financiero Personal Avanzado');
      document.documentElement.lang = lang;
      if (userConfig) {
        userConfig.language = lang;
        await pfSaveUserProfile();
      }
      localStorage.setItem('plannerfi_language', lang);
    }

    function countryCurrency(country) {
      return ({AR:'ARS',UY:'UYU',CL:'CLP',BR:'BRL',US:'USD',ES:'EUR'})[country] || 'ARS';
    }
    function syncCurrencyByCountry() {
      const c=document.getElementById('profileCountryInput').value;
      document.getElementById('profileCurrencyInput').value=countryCurrency(c);
    }
    async function handleProfileUpdate(e) {
      e.preventDefault();
      userConfig = {
        name: document.getElementById('profileNameInput').value.trim(),
        birthday: document.getElementById('profileBirthdayInput').value,
        workStatus: document.getElementById('profileWorkStatusInput').value,
        email: document.getElementById('profileEmailInput').value.trim(),
        language: 'es',
        country: document.getElementById('profileCountryInput').value,
        currency: document.getElementById('profileCurrencyInput').value,
        photo: document.getElementById('profilePhotoInput').value || ''
      };
      await pfSaveUserProfile();
      changeLanguage(userConfig.language);
      updateUserProfileUI();
      alert('Perfil ' + currentProfile + ' actualizado de forma independiente.');
    }

    function handleAddFamilyMember(e) {
      e.preventDefault();
      const emailInput = document.getElementById('familyMemberEmail');
      const email = emailInput.value;
      if (!email) return;
      familyMembers.push({
        id: Date.now(),
        email
      });
      saveData();
      renderFamilyMembers();
      emailInput.value = '';
    }

    function renderFamilyMembers() {
      const list = document.getElementById('familyMembersList');
      list.innerHTML = '';
      if (familyMembers.length === 0) {
        list.innerHTML = `<p class="text-xs text-slate-400">No hay otros miembros autorizados agregados.</p>`;
        return;
      }
      familyMembers.forEach(m => {
        list.innerHTML += `
          <div class="flex justify-between items-center p-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-700/30 text-xs">
            <span><b>${m.email}</b> (Acceso autorizado al perfil familiar)</span>
            <button onclick="removeFamilyMember(${m.id})" class="text-rose-500 hover:underline">Quitar</button>
          </div>
        `;
      });
    }

    function removeFamilyMember(id) {
      familyMembers = familyMembers.filter(m => m.id !== id);
      saveData();
      renderFamilyMembers();
    }

    function switchTab(tabId) {
      document.querySelectorAll('.tab-content').forEach(el => el.classList.add('hidden'));
      document.getElementById('tab-' + tabId).classList.remove('hidden');
      document.querySelectorAll('aside nav button').forEach(btn => {
        btn.className = "w-full flex items-center space-x-3 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-sm font-medium transition text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-600";
      });
      document.getElementById('nav-' + tabId).className = "w-full flex items-center space-x-3 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold transition bg-brand-500 text-white shadow-sm shadow-brand-500/30";
      
      if (tabId === 'dashboard') {
        renderCharts();
      }
      if (tabId === 'work') {
        renderWorkModule();
      }
      if (tabId === 'models') {
        renderModels();
      }
    }

    function switchProfile(profile) {
      if (!['Personal','Familiar','Negocio'].includes(profile)) return;
      currentProfile = profile;
      document.getElementById('currentProfileBadge').innerText = 'Perfil: ' + profile;
      // Se recarga TODO desde el espacio independiente del perfil seleccionado.
      enterPlannerFi();
    }

    async function saveData() {
      try {
        const firebaseUser = await pfGetFirebaseUser();
        await pfWriteSection(firebaseUser.uid, 'financial', {
          transactions: Array.isArray(transactions) ? transactions : [],
          debts: Array.isArray(debts) ? debts : [],
          savedModels: Array.isArray(savedModels) ? savedModels : [],
          savedModelFiles: Array.isArray(savedModelFiles) ? savedModelFiles : [],
          familyMembers: Array.isArray(familyMembers) ? familyMembers : []
        });
        return true;
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('Error al guardar datos en Firestore:', error);
        alert('No se pudieron guardar los datos en la cuenta. Revisá tu conexión y las reglas de Firebase.');
        return false;
      }
    }

    async function persistSavedModels() {
      return await saveData();
    }

    async function saveWorkData() {
      try {
        const firebaseUser = await pfGetFirebaseUser();
        await pfWriteSection(firebaseUser.uid, 'work', {
          workSettings: workSettings || {},
          workDays: Array.isArray(workDays) ? workDays : [],
          salaryReceipts: Array.isArray(salaryReceipts) ? salaryReceipts : [],
          workNotes: Array.isArray(workNotes) ? workNotes : []
        });
        return true;
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('Error al guardar trabajo en Firestore:', error);
        alert('No se pudieron guardar los datos laborales en la cuenta.');
        return false;
      }
    }

    function updateAll() {
      renderKPIs();
      renderTransactions();
      renderDebts();
      renderModels();
      if (!document.getElementById('tab-dashboard').classList.contains('hidden')) {
        renderCharts();
      }
      if (!document.getElementById('tab-work').classList.contains('hidden')) {
        renderWorkModule();
      }
    }

    function renderKPIs() {
      const list = Array.isArray(transactions) ? transactions : [];

      const totalIncome = list.reduce((total, t) => {
        return total + (String(t?.type || '').trim() === 'Ingreso' ? (Number(t?.amount) || 0) : 0);
      }, 0);

      const totalExpense = list.reduce((total, t) => {
        return total + (String(t?.type || '').trim() === 'Gasto' ? (Number(t?.amount) || 0) : 0);
      }, 0);

      const balance = totalIncome - totalExpense;
      const savings = Math.max(0, totalIncome * 0.20);

      const money = value => '$' + Number(value || 0).toLocaleString('es-AR', {
        minimumFractionDigits: 2,
        maximumFractionDigits: 2
      });

      const setValue = (id, value) => {
        const element = document.getElementById(id);
        if (element) element.textContent = money(value);
      };

      setValue('kpiIncome', totalIncome);
      setValue('kpiExpenses', totalExpense);
      setValue('kpiSavings', savings);
      setValue('sidebarBalance', balance);
      setValue('sidebarSavings', savings);
    }

    // Funciones del Módulo de Trabajo y Sueldo
    function handleWorkSettingsSubmit(e) {
      e.preventDefault();
      workSettings = {
        companyName: document.getElementById('wsCompanyName').value,
        jobTitle: document.getElementById('wsJobTitle').value,
        payType: document.getElementById('wsPayType').value,
        baseSalary: parseFloat(document.getElementById('wsBaseSalary').value) || 0,
        hourlyRate: parseFloat(document.getElementById('wsHourlyRate').value) || 0,
        normalHoursPerDay: parseFloat(document.getElementById('wsNormalHoursPerDay').value) || 8,
        overtime50Rate: parseFloat(document.getElementById('wsOvertime50Rate').value) || 50,
        overtime100Rate: parseFloat(document.getElementById('wsOvertime100Rate').value) || 100
      };
      saveWorkData();
      closeModal('workSettingsModal');
      renderWorkModule();
      alert('Configuración laboral guardada con éxito.');
    }

    function toggleNonWorkingDay() {
      const non = document.getElementById('wdStatus').value === 'Día no laborable';
      ['wdNormal','wd50','wd100'].forEach(id => { const el=document.getElementById(id); el.disabled=non; if(non) el.value=0; });
    }

    function handleWorkDaySubmit(e) {
      e.preventDefault();
      const status = document.getElementById('wdStatus').value;
      const isNonWorking = status === 'Día no laborable';
      const newDay = {
        id: Date.now(),
        date: document.getElementById('wdDate').value,
        status,
        normal: isNonWorking ? 0 : (parseFloat(document.getElementById('wdNormal').value) || 0),
        extra50: isNonWorking ? 0 : (parseFloat(document.getElementById('wd50').value) || 0),
        extra100: isNonWorking ? 0 : (parseFloat(document.getElementById('wd100').value) || 0),
        details: document.getElementById('wdDetails').value
      };
      workDays.unshift(newDay);
      saveWorkData();
      closeModal('workDayModal');
      renderWorkModule();
      e.target.reset();
    }

    function deleteWorkDay(id) {
      workDays = workDays.filter(d => d.id !== id);
      saveWorkData();
      renderWorkModule();
    }

    function handleSalaryReceiptSubmit(e) {
      e.preventDefault();
      const title = document.getElementById('srTitle').value;
      const type = document.getElementById('srType').value;
      const date = document.getElementById('srDate').value;
      const gross = parseFloat(document.getElementById('srGross').value) || 0;
      const discounts = parseFloat(document.getElementById('srDiscounts').value) || 0;
      const net = parseFloat(document.getElementById('srNet').value) || 0;
      const fileInput = document.getElementById('srFile');
      const fileName = fileInput.files[0] ? fileInput.files[0].name : 'Sin archivo';
      const addAsIncome = document.getElementById('srAddAsIncome').checked;

      const newReceipt = {
        id: Date.now(),
        title,
        type,
        date,
        gross,
        discounts,
        net,
        fileName
      };

      salaryReceipts.unshift(newReceipt);

      // Si el usuario marcó registrar como ingreso financiero
      if (addAsIncome) {
        transactions.unshift({
          id: Date.now() + 1,
          date: date || new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0],
          desc: `Pago laboral - ${title}`,
          category: 'Sueldo/Ingreso',
          type: 'Ingreso',
          amount: net
        });
      }

      saveData();
      saveWorkData();
      closeModal('salaryReceiptModal');
      renderWorkModule();
      updateAll();
      e.target.reset();
      alert('Recibo guardado correctamente.');
    }

    function deleteSalaryReceipt(id) {
      salaryReceipts = salaryReceipts.filter(r => r.id !== id);
      saveWorkData();
      renderWorkModule();
    }

    function handleWorkNoteSubmit(e) {
      e.preventDefault();
      const tag = document.getElementById('wnTag').value;
      const text = document.getElementById('wnText').value;
      workNotes.unshift({ id: Date.now(), tag, text });
      saveWorkData();
      closeModal('workNoteModal');
      renderWorkModule();
      e.target.reset();
    }

    function deleteWorkNote(id) {
      workNotes = workNotes.filter(n => n.id !== id);
      saveWorkData();
      renderWorkModule();
    }

    function renderWorkModule() {
      // 1. Calcular Totales Laborales
      let totalNormal = 0;
      let total50 = 0;
      let total100 = 0;
      let daysCount = workDays.length;

      workDays.forEach(d => {
        totalNormal += Number(d.normal) || 0;
        total50 += Number(d.extra50) || 0;
        total100 += Number(d.extra100) || 0;
      });

      let totalHours = totalNormal + total50 + total100;
      let hourlyRate = workSettings.hourlyRate || 5000;
      let rate50 = hourlyRate * (1 + (workSettings.overtime50Rate || 50) / 100);
      let rate100 = hourlyRate * (1 + (workSettings.overtime100Rate || 100) / 100);

      let estimatedPay = (totalNormal * hourlyRate) + (total50 * rate50) + (total100 * rate100);
      let receivedPay = salaryReceipts.reduce((acc, r) => acc + Number(r.net), 0);
      let payDiff = estimatedPay - receivedPay;

      // Renderizar KPIs laborales
      document.getElementById('kpiWorkHours').innerText = totalHours + ' h';
      document.getElementById('kpiWorkNormalHours').innerText = totalNormal + ' h';
      document.getElementById('kpiWork50Hours').innerText = total50 + ' h';
      document.getElementById('kpiWork100Hours').innerText = total100 + ' h';
      document.getElementById('kpiWorkDaysCount').innerText = daysCount;
      document.getElementById('kpiWorkEstimatedPay').innerText = '$' + estimatedPay.toLocaleString();
      document.getElementById('kpiWorkReceivedPay').innerText = '$' + receivedPay.toLocaleString();
      
      const diffEl = document.getElementById('kpiWorkPayDiff');
      diffEl.innerText = '$' + payDiff.toLocaleString();
      diffEl.className = `text-xl font-black mt-1 ${payDiff > 0 ? 'text-amber-500' : 'text-emerald-500'}`;

      // Análisis de Pago
      let basePayCalc = totalNormal * hourlyRate;
      let extrasPayCalc = (total50 * rate50) + (total100 * rate100);
      let grossPayCalc = basePayCalc + extrasPayCalc;

      document.getElementById('analysisBasePay').innerText = '$' + basePayCalc.toLocaleString();
      document.getElementById('analysisExtrasPay').innerText = '$' + extrasPayCalc.toLocaleString();
      document.getElementById('analysisGrossPay').innerText = '$' + grossPayCalc.toLocaleString();
      document.getElementById('analysisNetPay').innerText = '$' + receivedPay.toLocaleString();

      if (Math.abs(payDiff) > 10000 && receivedPay > 0) {
        document.getElementById('workAnalysisWarning').classList.remove('hidden');
      } else {
        document.getElementById('workAnalysisWarning').classList.add('hidden');
      }

      // Renderizar Tabla de Jornadas
      const daysTbody = document.getElementById('workDaysTableBody');
      daysTbody.innerHTML = '';
      if (workDays.length === 0) {
        daysTbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="9" class="py-4 text-center text-slate-400 text-xs">No hay jornadas registradas.</td></tr>`;
      } else {
        workDays.forEach(d => {
          let dayTotalH = Number(d.normal) + Number(d.extra50) + Number(d.extra100);
          let dayEst = (Number(d.normal) * hourlyRate) + (Number(d.extra50) * rate50) + (Number(d.extra100) * rate100);
          daysTbody.innerHTML += `
            <tr class="hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-700/30 transition text-xs">
              <td class="py-3 px-3 font-medium">${d.date}</td>
              <td class="py-3 px-3"><span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full font-semibold bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-700 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">${d.status}</span></td>
              <td class="py-3 px-3 text-center">${d.normal}h</td>
              <td class="py-3 px-3 text-center text-emerald-600 font-semibold">${d.extra50}h</td>
              <td class="py-3 px-3 text-center text-blue-600 font-semibold">${d.extra100}h</td>
              <td class="py-3 px-3 text-center font-bold">${dayTotalH}h</td>
              <td class="py-3 px-3 text-right font-bold text-brand-600">$${dayEst.toLocaleString()}</td>
              <td class="py-3 px-3 text-slate-500 truncate max-w-[120px]">${d.details || '-'}</td>
              <td class="py-3 px-3 text-center">
                <button onclick="deleteWorkDay(${d.id})" class="text-rose-500 hover:bg-rose-50 p-1 rounded"><i data-lucide="trash-2" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i></button>
              </td>
            </tr>
          `;
        });
      }

      // Renderizar Recibos
      const receiptsContainer = document.getElementById('salaryReceiptsContainer');
      receiptsContainer.innerHTML = '';
      if (salaryReceipts.length === 0) {
        receiptsContainer.innerHTML = `<p class="text-slate-400 text-xs col-span-3">No hay recibos cargados.</p>`;
      } else {
        salaryReceipts.forEach(r => {
          receiptsContainer.innerHTML += `
            <div class="p-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-700/30 space-y-2">
              <div class="flex justify-between items-center">
                <h5 class="font-bold text-sm text-slate-900 dark:text-white">${r.title}</h5>
                <span class="text-[10px] bg-brand-50 dark:bg-brand-900/30 text-brand-600 px-2 py-0.5 rounded font-semibold">${r.type}</span>
              </div>
              <div class="text-xs space-y-1 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300">
                <div>Bruto: <b>$${Number(r.gross).toLocaleString()}</b></div>
                <div>Descuentos: <b class="text-rose-500">-$${Number(r.discounts).toLocaleString()}</b></div>
                <div>Neto: <b class="text-emerald-600 text-sm">$${Number(r.net).toLocaleString()}</b></div>
              </div>
              <div class="pt-2 border-t border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 flex justify-between items-center text-xs text-slate-400">
                <span>📁 ${r.fileName}</span>
                <button onclick="deleteSalaryReceipt(${r.id})" class="text-rose-500 hover:underline">Eliminar</button>
              </div>
            </div>
          `;
        });
      }

      // Tabla Comparativa de Recibos
      const compTbody = document.getElementById('receiptsComparisonTableBody');
      compTbody.innerHTML = '';
      if (salaryReceipts.length === 0) {
        compTbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="6" class="py-3 text-center text-slate-400">No hay datos para comparar.</td></tr>`;
      } else {
        for (let i = 0; i < salaryReceipts.length; i++) {
          let curr = salaryReceipts[i];
          let prev = salaryReceipts[i + 1];
          let variation = '-';
          if (prev) {
            let diff = ((curr.net - prev.net) / prev.net) * 100;
            variation = `<span class="${diff >= 0 ? 'text-emerald-500 font-bold' : 'text-rose-500 font-bold'}">${diff >= 0 ? '+' : ''}${diff.toFixed(1)}%</span>`;
          }
          compTbody.innerHTML += `
            <tr class="hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-700/30 transition">
              <td class="py-2.5 px-3 font-medium">${curr.title}</td>
              <td class="py-2.5 px-3">${curr.type}</td>
              <td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-right">$${Number(curr.gross).toLocaleString()}</td>
              <td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-right text-rose-500">-$${Number(curr.discounts).toLocaleString()}</td>
              <td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-right font-bold text-emerald-600">$${Number(curr.net).toLocaleString()}</td>
              <td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-center">${variation}</td>
            </tr>
          `;
        }
      }

      // Renderizar Notas
      const notesContainer = document.getElementById('workNotesContainer');
      notesContainer.innerHTML = '';
      if (workNotes.length === 0) {
        notesContainer.innerHTML = `<p class="text-slate-400 text-xs">No hay notas registradas.</p>`;
      } else {
        workNotes.forEach(n => {
          notesContainer.innerHTML += `
            <div class="p-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-700/30 flex justify-between items-start text-xs">
              <div>
                <span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-brand-50 dark:bg-brand-900/30 text-brand-600 font-bold text-[10px]">${n.tag}</span>
                <p class="mt-1.5 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">${n.text}</p>
              </div>
              <button onclick="deleteWorkNote(${n.id})" class="text-rose-500 hover:underline">Quitar</button>
            </div>
          `;
        });
      }

      // Resumen y Análisis Automático Dinámico
      document.getElementById('automaticWorkAnalysisText').innerHTML = `
        <p>• Durante este período registraste <b>${daysCount} días trabajados</b> acumulando un total de <b>${totalHours} horas</b>.</p>
        <p>• De esas horas, <b>${totalNormal}h</b> fueron normales, <b>${total50}h</b> con recargo del 50% y <b>${total100}h</b> con recargo del 100%.</p>
        <p>• El pago estimado calculado por el sistema asciende a <b>$${estimatedPay.toLocaleString()}</b>, mientras que el total recibido en tus recibos netos es de <b>$${receivedPay.toLocaleString()}</b>.</p>
        <p>• La diferencia actual es de <b class="${payDiff > 0 ? 'text-amber-500' : 'text-emerald-500'}">$${payDiff.toLocaleString()}</b>.</p>
      `;

      renderWorkCharts();
      lucide.createIcons();
    }

    function renderWorkCharts() {
      const pieCanvas=document.getElementById('workHoursPieChart');
      const salaryCanvas=document.getElementById('salaryEvolutionChart');
      if(!pieCanvas || !salaryCanvas || !window.Chart) return;
      if(workHoursPieChartInstance) workHoursPieChartInstance.destroy();
      if(salaryEvolutionChartInstance) salaryEvolutionChartInstance.destroy();
      const theme=pfChartTheme();
      const totalNormal=workDays.reduce((acc,d)=>acc+Number(d.normal),0);
      const total50=workDays.reduce((acc,d)=>acc+Number(d.extra50),0);
      const total100=workDays.reduce((acc,d)=>acc+Number(d.extra100),0);
      const totalHours=totalNormal+total50+total100;
      const money=v=>new Intl.NumberFormat('es-AR',{minimumFractionDigits:2,maximumFractionDigits:2}).format(v);
      const pieCtx=pieCanvas.getContext('2d'), salaryCtx=salaryCanvas.getContext('2d');
      workHoursPieChartInstance=new Chart(pieCtx,{type:'doughnut',data:{labels:['Horas Normales','Horas 50%','Horas 100%'],datasets:[{data:[totalNormal,total50,total100],backgroundColor:['#3b82f6','#10b981','#f59e0b'],borderWidth:3,borderColor:theme.surface,hoverOffset:7,spacing:2}]},options:{responsive:true,maintainAspectRatio:false,cutout:'72%',animation:{duration:750,easing:'easeOutQuart'},plugins:{legend:{position:'bottom',labels:{color:theme.text,usePointStyle:true,pointStyle:'circle',padding:14,font:{size:10,weight:'700'}}},tooltip:{backgroundColor:theme.surface,titleColor:theme.text,bodyColor:theme.text,borderColor:theme.grid,borderWidth:1,padding:12,cornerRadius:12,callbacks:{label:c=>` ${c.label}: ${c.raw} h`}},pfCenterText:{text:`${totalHours.toFixed(1)} h`,sub:'TOTAL',color:theme.text,subColor:theme.muted}}}});
      const grad=pfGradient(salaryCtx,'#34d399','#059669');
      const receiptLabels=salaryReceipts.slice().reverse().map(r=>r.title);
      const receiptValues=salaryReceipts.slice().reverse().map(r=>r.net);
      salaryEvolutionChartInstance=new Chart(salaryCtx,{type:'bar',data:{labels:receiptLabels.length?receiptLabels:['Sin recibos'],datasets:[{label:'Sueldo Neto ($)',data:receiptValues.length?receiptValues:[0],backgroundColor:grad,borderRadius:10,borderSkipped:false,maxBarThickness:46}]},options:{responsive:true,maintainAspectRatio:false,animation:{duration:700,easing:'easeOutQuart'},plugins:{legend:{display:false},tooltip:{backgroundColor:theme.surface,titleColor:theme.text,bodyColor:theme.text,borderColor:theme.grid,borderWidth:1,padding:12,cornerRadius:12,callbacks:{label:c=>` $${money(c.raw)}`}}},scales:{x:{grid:{display:false},border:{display:false},ticks:{color:theme.text,font:{size:9,weight:'800'},maxRotation:0}},y:{beginAtZero:true,grid:{color:theme.grid},border:{display:false},ticks:{color:theme.muted,font:{size:9},callback:v=>`$${money(v)}`}}}}});
    }

    // Asistente IA Laboral Local
    function askWorkAssistant(question) {
      const chatBox = document.getElementById('workChatMessages');
      
      // Agregar mensaje del usuario
      chatBox.innerHTML += `
        <div class="self-end bg-brand-600 text-white p-3 rounded-2xl rounded-tr-none text-xs max-w-md shadow-sm">
          ${question}
        </div>
      `;

      // Calcular respuesta local inteligente basada en los datos reales del usuario
      setTimeout(() => {
        let response = '';
        let totalNormal = workDays.reduce((acc, d) => acc + Number(d.normal), 0);
        let total50 = workDays.reduce((acc, d) => acc + Number(d.extra50), 0);
        let total100 = workDays.reduce((acc, d) => acc + Number(d.extra100), 0);
        let totalHours = totalNormal + total50 + total100;
        let hourlyRate = workSettings.hourlyRate || 5000;
        let rate50 = hourlyRate * 1.5;
        let rate100 = hourlyRate * 2.0;
        let estimatedPay = (totalNormal * hourlyRate) + (total50 * rate50) + (total100 * rate100);
        let receivedPay = salaryReceipts.reduce((acc, r) => acc + Number(r.net), 0);

        if (question.includes('horas')) {
          response = `Tenés registradas un total de <b>${totalHours} horas</b> (${totalNormal}h normales, ${total50}h al 50% y ${total100}h al 100%) en ${workDays.length} jornadas laborales.`;
        } else if (question.includes('pago fue diferente') || question.includes('diferencia')) {
          let diff = estimatedPay - receivedPay;
          response = `El pago estimado según tus horas es de $${estimatedPay.toLocaleString()}, mientras que el neto en recibos es $${receivedPay.toLocaleString()}. La diferencia es de $${diff.toLocaleString()}, lo cual suele explicarse por aportes jubilatorios, obra social o descuentos de ley.`;
        } else if (question.includes('gané por hora')) {
          response = `Tu valor de hora configurado es de <b>$${hourlyRate.toLocaleString()}</b> (hora normal), con recargos a $${rate50.toLocaleString()} (50%) y $${rate100.toLocaleString()} (100%).`;
        } else if (question.includes('recibos')) {
          response = `Tenés <b>${salaryReceipts.length} recibos</b> registrados. El último recibo registrado ("${salaryReceipts[0] ? salaryReceipts[0].title : 'N/A'}") tiene un sueldo neto de $${salaryReceipts[0] ? Number(salaryReceipts[0].net).toLocaleString() : 0}.`;
        } else {
          response = `Analizando tus datos laborales actuales: Tenés ${workDays.length} jornadas registradas y un total de ${salaryReceipts.length} recibos de sueldo. ¿Te gustaría consultar el detalle de horas o comparación salarial?`;
        }

        chatBox.innerHTML += `
          <div class="self-start bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 p-3 rounded-2xl rounded-tl-none border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 text-xs max-w-md shadow-sm">
            ${response}
          </div>
        `;
        chatBox.scrollTop = chatBox.scrollHeight;
      }, 400);

      chatBox.scrollTop = chatBox.scrollHeight;
    }

    function handleWorkChatSubmit(e) {
      e.preventDefault();
      const input = document.getElementById('workChatInput');
      const text = input.value.trim();
      if (!text) return;
      askWorkAssistant(text);
      input.value = '';
    }

    // Funciones existentes de transacciones, deudas, etc.
    function renderTransactions() {
      const filter = document.getElementById('filterCategory').value;
      const tbody = document.getElementById('transactionsTableBody');
      tbody.innerHTML = '';
      let filtered = transactions;
      if (filter !== 'All') {
        filtered = transactions.filter(t => t.category === filter);
      }
      if (filtered.length === 0) {
        tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="6" class="py-4 text-center text-slate-400">No hay movimientos registrados.</td></tr>`;
        return;
      }
      filtered.forEach(t => {
        tbody.innerHTML += `
          <tr class="hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-700/30 transition">
            <td class="py-3 px-4">${t.date}</td>
            <td class="py-3 px-4 font-medium">${t.desc}</td>
            <td class="py-3 px-4"><span class="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-semibold bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-700">${t.category}</span></td>
            <td class="py-3 px-4"><span class="font-bold ${t.type === 'Ingreso' ? 'text-emerald-500':'text-rose-500'}">${t.type}</span></td>
            <td class="py-3 px-4 text-right font-bold ${t.type === 'Ingreso' ? 'text-emerald-500':'text-slate-800 dark:text-white'}">$${Number(t.amount).toLocaleString()}</td>
            <td class="py-3 px-4 text-center">
              <button onclick="deleteTransaction(${t.id})" class="text-rose-500 hover:bg-rose-50 p-1.5 rounded-lg"><i data-lucide="trash-2" class="w-4 h-4"></i></button>
            </td>
          </tr>
        `;
      });
      lucide.createIcons();
    }

    function handleTransactionSubmit(e) {
      e.preventDefault();

      const amount = Number(document.getElementById('transAmount').value);
      const type = document.getElementById('transType').value;

      if (!Number.isFinite(amount) || amount <= 0) {
        alert('Ingresá un monto válido mayor que 0.');
        return;
      }

      if (type !== 'Ingreso' && type !== 'Gasto') {
        alert('Seleccioná si el movimiento es un Ingreso o un Gasto.');
        return;
      }

      const newT = {
        id: Date.now() + Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000),
        date: new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0],
        desc: document.getElementById('transDesc').value.trim(),
        amount: amount,
        type: type,
        category: document.getElementById('transCategory').value
      };

      transactions.unshift(newT);
      saveData();
      updateAll();
      closeModal('transactionModal');
      e.target.reset();
    }

    function deleteTransaction(id) {
      transactions = transactions.filter(t => t.id !== id);
      saveData();
      updateAll();
    }

    function renderDebts() {
      const container = document.getElementById('debtsContainer');
      container.innerHTML = '';
      if (debts.length === 0) {
        container.innerHTML = `<p class="text-slate-400 text-sm col-span-2">No hay obligaciones registradas.</p>`;
        return;
      }
      debts.forEach(d => {
        container.innerHTML += `
          <div class="p-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-700/30 flex justify-between items-center">
            <div>
              <h4 class="font-bold text-sm">${d.title} <span class="text-brand-600 ml-2">($${Number(d.amount).toLocaleString()})</span></h4>
              <p class="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-0.5">Vencimiento: ${d.date}</p>
              ${d.paid ? `<span class="text-xs text-emerald-500 font-bold mt-1 inline-block">Pagado · Único comprobante: ${escapeHtml(d.receipt || 'Archivo')}</span>${d.paymentVerification ? `<p class="text-[11px] text-slate-400 mt-1">Verificación: ${d.paymentVerification.score}% · ${escapeHtml(d.paymentVerification.method || '')}</p>` : ''}` : `<span class="text-xs text-amber-500 font-bold mt-1 inline-block">Pendiente de pago</span>`}
            </div>
            <div>
              ${!d.paid ? `<button onclick="openPayDebtModal(${d.id})" class="text-xs bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white font-bold px-3 py-2 rounded-lg shadow">Pagar y Adjuntar</button>` : `<button onclick="deleteDebt(${d.id})" class="text-xs text-rose-500 hover:underline">Eliminar</button>`}
            </div>
          </div>
        `;
      });
    }

    function handleDebtSubmit(e) {
      e.preventDefault();
      const newD = {
        id: Date.now(),
        title: document.getElementById('debtTitle').value,
        amount: parseFloat(document.getElementById('debtAmount').value),
        date: document.getElementById('debtDate').value,
        paid: false,
        receipt: null
      };
      debts.push(newD);
      saveData();
      renderDebts();
      closeModal('debtModal');
      e.target.reset();
    }

    function openPayDebtModal(id) {
      document.getElementById('payingDebtId').value = id;
      openModal('payDebtModal');
    }

    async function handlePayDebtSubmit(e) {
      e.preventDefault();
      const id = parseInt(document.getElementById('payingDebtId').value);
      const fileInput = document.getElementById('debtReceiptFile');
      const file = fileInput.files[0];
      const debt = debts.find(d => d.id === id);
      const status = document.getElementById('receiptVerificationStatus');
      if (!file || !debt) return;

      status.className = 'text-xs p-3 rounded-xl border bg-blue-50 border-blue-200 text-blue-700';
      status.textContent = 'Analizando comprobante y verificando coincidencia con la deuda...';
      status.classList.remove('hidden');

      const declaredAmount = Number(document.getElementById('receiptPaymentAmount').value || 0);
      const reference = document.getElementById('receiptReference').value.trim();
      const result = await verifyDebtReceipt(file, debt, declaredAmount, reference);

      if (!result.approved) {
        status.className = 'text-xs p-3 rounded-xl border bg-rose-50 border-rose-200 text-rose-700';
        status.innerHTML = `<b>Verificación rechazada.</b> ${result.message}`;
        return;
      }

      status.className = 'text-xs p-3 rounded-xl border bg-emerald-50 border-emerald-200 text-emerald-700';
      status.innerHTML = `<b>Comprobante verificado.</b> ${result.message}`;
      debts = debts.map(d => d.id === id ? {
        ...d, paid: true, receipt: file.name,
        paymentVerification: { score: result.score, checkedAt: new Date().toISOString(), amount: declaredAmount, reference, method: result.method, fileType: file.type, fileSize: file.size }
      } : d);
      saveData(); updateAll();
      setTimeout(() => { closeModal('payDebtModal'); e.target.reset(); status.classList.add('hidden'); }, 900);
    }

    async function verifyDebtReceipt(file, debt, declaredAmount, reference) {
      const amountMatches = Math.abs(declaredAmount - Number(debt.amount)) < 0.01;
      if (!amountMatches) return { approved:false, score:0, method:'amount', message:`El monto informado ($${declaredAmount.toLocaleString()}) no coincide con la deuda ($${Number(debt.amount).toLocaleString()}).` };

      let text = '';
      let ocrUsed = false;
      if (file.type.startsWith('image/') && window.Tesseract) {
        try {
          const { data } = await Tesseract.recognize(file, 'spa+eng', { logger: () => {} });
          text = (data.text || '').toLowerCase();
          ocrUsed = true;
        } catch (err) { text = ''; }
      }

      const normalizedTitle = debt.title.toLowerCase().split(/\s+/).filter(w => w.length >= 4);
      const titleMatch = normalizedTitle.some(word => text.includes(word));
      const amountTokens = [
        String(Number(debt.amount)),
        Number(debt.amount).toLocaleString('es-AR'),
        Number(debt.amount).toLocaleString('en-US')
      ].map(x => x.replace(/\./g,'').replace(/,/g,''));
      const compactText = text.replace(/\./g,'').replace(/,/g,'').replace(/\s/g,'');
      const amountInReceipt = amountTokens.some(x => compactText.includes(x));

      // Para imágenes se exige evidencia OCR del concepto o monto; para PDF se requiere confirmación declarada + referencia.
      if (ocrUsed && text && !(titleMatch || amountInReceipt)) {
        return { approved:false, score:45, method:'OCR', message:'La lectura inteligente no encontró coincidencias suficientes entre el comprobante y el concepto o monto de la deuda. Revisá el archivo.' };
      }
      if (!ocrUsed && file.type === 'application/pdf' && !reference) {
        return { approved:false, score:55, method:'manual', message:'Para PDF sin lectura OCR ingresá una referencia de operación para reforzar la validación.' };
      }

      let score = 60 + (amountMatches ? 25 : 0) + (titleMatch ? 10 : 0) + (amountInReceipt ? 5 : 0);
      return { approved:true, score:Math.min(score,100), method:ocrUsed ? 'OCR + reglas de coincidencia' : 'reglas de coincidencia', message:`Coincidencia validada con ${Math.min(score,100)}% de confianza (${ocrUsed ? 'texto extraído del comprobante' : 'monto y referencia declarados'}).` };
    }

    function deleteDebt(id) {
      debts = debts.filter(d => d.id !== id);
      saveData();
      renderDebts();
    }

    function renderModels() {
      const list = document.getElementById('savedModelsList');
      list.innerHTML = '';
      const hasPlans = savedModels.length > 0;
      const hasFiles = savedModelFiles.length > 0;
      if (!hasPlans && !hasFiles) {
        list.innerHTML = `<p class="text-slate-400 text-sm">No hay planes ni archivos guardados todavía.</p>`;
        return;
      }

      if (hasPlans) {
        list.innerHTML += `<h4 class="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400">Planes guardados</h4>`;
        savedModels.forEach(m => {
          list.innerHTML += `
            <div class="p-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-700/30 space-y-2">
              <div class="flex justify-between items-center gap-3">
                <h4 class="font-bold text-sm text-brand-600">${escapeHtml(m.name)}</h4>
                <div class="flex gap-3 items-center"><span class="text-xs text-slate-400">${m.date}</span><button onclick="renameSavedModel(${m.id})" class="text-xs text-slate-500 font-bold hover:underline">Renombrar</button><button onclick="downloadPlanModel(${m.id})" class="text-xs text-brand-600 font-bold hover:underline">Descargar</button></div>
              </div>
              <div class="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 text-xs bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 p-3 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700">
                <div>Perfil: <b>${m.profile}</b></div>
                <div>Movimientos: <b>${m.transCount}</b></div>
                <div>Balance: <b class="text-emerald-500">$${Number(m.balance || 0).toLocaleString()}</b></div>
                <div class="col-span-3 text-slate-400">Tipo: <b class="text-brand-600">${m.type || 'Plan financiero'}</b>${m.period ? ' · Período: <b>' + m.period + '</b>' : ''}</div>
              </div>
            </div>`;
        });
      }

      if (hasFiles) {
        list.innerHTML += `<h4 class="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400 pt-3">Archivos guardados</h4>`;
        savedModelFiles.forEach(f => {
          list.innerHTML += `
            <div class="p-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
              <div class="flex items-center gap-3 min-w-0">
                <div class="p-2 rounded-lg bg-brand-50 dark:bg-brand-900/30 text-brand-600"><i data-lucide="file" class="w-5 h-5"></i></div>
                <div class="min-w-0"><h4 class="font-bold text-sm truncate">${escapeHtml(f.name)}</h4><p class="text-xs text-slate-400">${f.type || 'Archivo'} · ${formatBytes(f.size)} · ${f.date}</p></div>
              </div>
              <div class="flex gap-2 shrink-0">
                <button onclick="downloadModelFile(${f.id})" class="text-xs bg-brand-600 text-white px-3 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold">Ver / Descargar</button>
                <button onclick="deleteModelFile(${f.id})" class="text-xs text-rose-500 font-bold px-2">Eliminar</button>
              </div>
            </div>`;
        });
      }
      lucide.createIcons();
    }

    function escapeHtml(value) {
      return String(value || '').replace(/[&<>"']/g, c => ({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#039;'}[c]));
    }
    function formatBytes(bytes) {
      if (!bytes) return '0 B';
      const units = ['B','KB','MB']; const i = Math.min(Math.floor(Math.log(bytes)/Math.log(1024)), 2);
      return (bytes / Math.pow(1024,i)).toFixed(i ? 1 : 0) + ' ' + units[i];
    }

    async function saveModelFile(event) {
      const files = [...event.target.files];
      if (!files.length) return;
      const firebaseUser = window.plannerFiFirebaseUser || await window.firebaseAuthReady;
      if (!firebaseUser?.uid) {
        alert('Iniciá sesión para guardar archivos.');
        event.target.value = '';
        return;
      }

      for (const file of files) {
        // Sin Firebase Storage, el archivo se guarda como Data URL dentro de Firestore.
        // Se limita el tamaño para evitar superar el límite de un documento de Firestore.
        if (file.size > 450 * 1024) {
          alert(`"${file.name}" supera 450 KB. En esta versión sin Storage los archivos deben ser pequeños.`);
          continue;
        }
        try {
          const id = `${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,9)}`;
          const dataUrl = await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
            const reader = new FileReader();
            reader.onload = () => resolve(reader.result);
            reader.onerror = () => reject(reader.error || new Error('No se pudo leer el archivo.'));
            reader.readAsDataURL(file);
          });

          savedModelFiles.unshift({
            id,
            name: file.name,
            type: file.type,
            size: file.size,
            date: new Date().toLocaleString('es-AR'),
            data: dataUrl
          });

          await saveData();
          renderModels();
        } catch (err) {
          console.error(err);
          alert(`No se pudo guardar "${file.name}".`);
        }
      }
      event.target.value = '';
    }

    async function downloadModelFile(id) {
      const f = savedModelFiles.find(x => x.id === id);
      if (!f) return;
      const url = f.data || f.url;
      if (!url) return alert('No se encontró el archivo guardado.');
      const a = document.createElement('a');
      a.href = url;
      a.target = '_blank';
      a.rel = 'noopener';
      a.download = f.name;
      document.body.appendChild(a);
      a.click();
      a.remove();
    }

    async function deleteModelFile(id) {
      if (!confirm('¿Eliminar este archivo guardado?')) return;
      savedModelFiles = savedModelFiles.filter(f => f.id !== id);
      await saveData();
      renderModels();
    }

    function downloadPlanModel(id) {
      const m = savedModels.find(x => x.id === id); if (!m) return;
      const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(m, null, 2)], {type:'application/json'});
      const a = document.createElement('a'); a.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob); a.download = `${m.name.replace(/[^\w-]+/g,'_')}.json`; a.click(); URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href);
    }

    function renameSavedModel(id) {
      const model = savedModels.find(m => m.id === id);
      if (!model) return;
      const newName = prompt('Modificar nombre del archivo guardado:', model.name);
      if (!newName || !newName.trim()) return;
      model.name = newName.trim();
      model.updatedAt = new Date().toLocaleString();
      const linkedFile = savedModelFiles.find(f => f.linkedModelId === id || f.id === id);
      if (linkedFile) {
        linkedFile.displayName = model.name;
        linkedFile.name = `${model.name}.json`;
      }
      saveData(); renderModels();
    }

    // Guarda un registro completo y además genera un archivo descargable visible en Modelos / Planes
    function saveArtifactInModels(model) {
      try {
        const artifactId = Date.now() + Math.floor(Math.random() * 100000);
        model.id = artifactId;
        model.fileId = artifactId;
        model.updatedAt = new Date().toLocaleString();
        savedModels.unshift(model);

        const filePayload = {
          name: model.name,
          type: model.type,
          period: model.period || null,
          profile: model.profile,
          date: model.date,
          snapshot: model.snapshot || {}
        };
        const json = JSON.stringify(filePayload, null, 2);
        const data = 'data:application/json;charset=utf-8,' + encodeURIComponent(json);
        savedModelFiles.unshift({
          id: artifactId,
          linkedModelId: artifactId,
          name: `${model.name}.json`,
          displayName: model.name,
          type: 'application/json',
          size: new Blob([json]).size,
          date: model.date,
          data,
          sourceType: model.type
        });

        if (!saveData()) throw new Error('No se pudo persistir');
        renderModels();
        return true;
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('Error guardando artefacto:', error);
        savedModels = savedModels.filter(m => m.id !== model.id);
        savedModelFiles = savedModelFiles.filter(f => f.id !== model.id);
        alert('No se pudo guardar el archivo en Modelos / Planes.');
        return false;
      }
    }

    function saveWorkAnalysis() {
      const period = prompt('¿Cómo querés guardar este análisis laboral? Escribí: Mensual o Quincenal', 'Mensual');
      if (!period) return;
      const normalizedPeriod = period.trim().toLowerCase().includes('quin') ? 'Quincenal' : 'Mensual';
      const defaultName = `Análisis Laboral ${normalizedPeriod} - ${new Date().toLocaleDateString()}`;
      const name = prompt('Elegí el nombre del archivo de análisis:', defaultName);
      if (!name || !name.trim()) return;

      let totalNormal = 0, total50 = 0, total100 = 0;
      workDays.forEach(d => { totalNormal += Number(d.normal)||0; total50 += Number(d.extra50)||0; total100 += Number(d.extra100)||0; });
      const hourlyRate = Number(workSettings.hourlyRate) || 0;
      const rate50 = hourlyRate * (1 + (Number(workSettings.overtime50Rate)||50)/100);
      const rate100 = hourlyRate * (1 + (Number(workSettings.overtime100Rate)||100)/100);
      const estimatedPay = totalNormal*hourlyRate + total50*rate50 + total100*rate100;
      const receivedPay = salaryReceipts.reduce((a,r)=>a+(Number(r.net)||0),0);

      const model = {
        name: name.trim(), profile: currentProfile, date: new Date().toLocaleString(),
        type: 'Análisis de Trabajo y Sueldo', period: normalizedPeriod, transCount: workDays.length, balance: estimatedPay - receivedPay,
        snapshot: {
          workSettings: {...workSettings}, workDays: [...workDays], salaryReceipts: [...salaryReceipts], workNotes: [...workNotes],
          analysis: { totalNormal, total50, total100, totalHours: totalNormal+total50+total100, estimatedPay, receivedPay, difference: estimatedPay-receivedPay }
        }
      };
      if (!saveArtifactInModels(model)) return;
      switchTab('models');
      alert(`Análisis ${normalizedPeriod.toLowerCase()} guardado correctamente y agregado a Modelos y Planes Guardados con Detalle.`);
    }

    function savePlanModel() {
      const modelName = prompt("Ingrese un nombre para guardar este Plan/Modelo detallado:", "Plan " + new Date().toLocaleDateString());
      if (!modelName || !modelName.trim()) return;
      let totalIncome = transactions.filter(t => t.type === 'Ingreso').reduce((acc, t) => acc + Number(t.amount), 0);
      let totalExpense = transactions.filter(t => t.type === 'Gasto').reduce((acc, t) => acc + Number(t.amount), 0);
      const model = {
        name: modelName.trim(), profile: currentProfile, date: new Date().toLocaleString(),
        type: 'Plan financiero del Dashboard', transCount: transactions.length, balance: totalIncome - totalExpense,
        snapshot: { transactions: [...transactions], debts: [...debts], workSettings: {...workSettings}, workDays: [...workDays], salaryReceipts: [...salaryReceipts], workNotes: [...workNotes] }
      };
      if (!saveArtifactInModels(model)) return;
      switchTab('models');
      alert("Plan guardado exitosamente y agregado a Modelos y Planes Guardados con Detalle.");
    }

    // Calculators/Simulators logic
    function calculateLoanSim() {
      const P = parseFloat(document.getElementById('simLoanAmount').value);
      const r = parseFloat(document.getElementById('simLoanRate').value) / 100 / 12;
      const n = parseInt(document.getElementById('simLoanMonths').value);
      const cuota = (P * r * Math.pow(1 + r, n)) / (Math.pow(1 + r, n) - 1);
      document.getElementById('simLoanResult').innerText = `Cuota Mensual: $${cuota.toFixed(2)}`;
    }

    function calculateInvSim() {
      const C = parseFloat(document.getElementById('simInvCapital').value);
      const r = parseFloat(document.getElementById('simInvRate').value) / 100;
      const t = parseInt(document.getElementById('simInvYears').value);
      const total = C * Math.pow(1 + r, t);
      document.getElementById('simInvResult').innerText = `Monto Final Proyectado: $${total.toFixed(2)}`;
    }

    function calculateRuleSim() {
      const income = parseFloat(document.getElementById('simRuleIncome').value);
      const needs = income * 0.50;
      const wants = income * 0.30;
      const savings = income * 0.20;
      document.getElementById('simRuleResult').innerHTML = `
        <div>Necesidades (50%): <b>$${needs.toFixed(2)}</b></div>
        <div>Deseos (30%): <b>$${wants.toFixed(2)}</b></div>
        <div>Ahorro (20%): <b>$${savings.toFixed(2)}</b></div>
      `;
    }

    // Charts Rendering — visual premium, misma fuente de datos y misma lógica
    function pfChartTheme(){
      const dark=document.documentElement.classList.contains('dark');
      return {
        text: dark ? '#cbd5e1' : '#475569',
        muted: dark ? '#64748b' : '#94a3b8',
        grid: dark ? 'rgba(148,163,184,.10)' : 'rgba(15,23,42,.07)',
        surface: dark ? '#1e293b' : '#ffffff'
      };
    }

    const pfCenterTextPlugin={
      id:'pfCenterText',
      afterDraw(chart,args,opts){
        if(!opts || !opts.text || chart.config.type!=='doughnut') return;
        const {ctx,chartArea}=chart;
        if(!chartArea) return;
        const x=(chartArea.left+chartArea.right)/2, y=(chartArea.top+chartArea.bottom)/2;
        ctx.save();
        ctx.textAlign='center'; ctx.textBaseline='middle';
        ctx.fillStyle=opts.color||'#0f172a'; ctx.font='900 18px Inter,system-ui,sans-serif';
        ctx.fillText(opts.text,x,y-5);
        ctx.fillStyle=opts.subColor||'#94a3b8'; ctx.font='700 9px Inter,system-ui,sans-serif';
        ctx.fillText(opts.sub||'',x,y+13);
        ctx.restore();
      }
    };
    if(window.Chart && !Chart.registry.plugins.get('pfCenterText')) Chart.register(pfCenterTextPlugin);

    function pfGradient(ctx,top,bottom){
      const g=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,0,0,ctx.canvas.height||260); g.addColorStop(0,top); g.addColorStop(1,bottom); return g;
    }

    function renderCharts() {
      const pieCanvas=document.getElementById('expensesPieChart');
      const barCanvas=document.getElementById('flowBarChart');
      if(!pieCanvas || !barCanvas || !window.Chart) return;
      if (pieChartInstance) pieChartInstance.destroy();
      if (barChartInstance) barChartInstance.destroy();
      const theme=pfChartTheme();
      const categories=['Alimentación','Vivienda','Transporte','Ocio','Salud','Suscripciones','Educación'];
      const catData=categories.map(cat=>transactions.filter(t=>t.category===cat && t.type==='Gasto').reduce((acc,t)=>acc+Number(t.amount),0));
      const totalExpense=catData.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0);
      const totalIncome=transactions.filter(t=>t.type==='Ingreso').reduce((acc,t)=>acc+Number(t.amount),0);
      const money=v=>new Intl.NumberFormat('es-AR',{minimumFractionDigits:2,maximumFractionDigits:2}).format(v);
      const pieCtx=pieCanvas.getContext('2d'), barCtx=barCanvas.getContext('2d');

      pieChartInstance=new Chart(pieCtx,{
        type:'doughnut',
        data:{labels:categories,datasets:[{
          data:catData,
          backgroundColor:['#10b981','#3b82f6','#f59e0b','#ef4444','#8b5cf6','#ec4899','#14b8a6'],
          borderWidth:3,borderColor:theme.surface,hoverOffset:7,spacing:2
        }]},
        options:{responsive:true,maintainAspectRatio:false,cutout:'72%',animation:{duration:750,easing:'easeOutQuart'},
          plugins:{legend:{position:'bottom',labels:{color:theme.text,usePointStyle:true,pointStyle:'circle',padding:14,font:{size:10,weight:'700'}}},
            tooltip:{backgroundColor:theme.surface,titleColor:theme.text,bodyColor:theme.text,borderColor:theme.grid,borderWidth:1,padding:12,cornerRadius:12,displayColors:true,
              callbacks:{label:c=>` ${c.label}: ${money(c.raw)}`}},
            pfCenterText:{text:money(totalExpense),sub:'GASTOS',color:theme.text,subColor:theme.muted}}
        }
      });

      const incomeGrad=pfGradient(barCtx,'#34d399','#059669');
      const expenseGrad=pfGradient(barCtx,'#fb7185','#e11d48');
      barChartInstance=new Chart(barCtx,{
        type:'bar',
        data:{labels:['Ingresos','Gastos'],datasets:[{label:'Monto ($)',data:[totalIncome,totalExpense],backgroundColor:[incomeGrad,expenseGrad],borderRadius:12,borderSkipped:false,maxBarThickness:64}]},
        options:{responsive:true,maintainAspectRatio:false,animation:{duration:700,easing:'easeOutQuart'},
          plugins:{legend:{display:false},tooltip:{backgroundColor:theme.surface,titleColor:theme.text,bodyColor:theme.text,borderColor:theme.grid,borderWidth:1,padding:12,cornerRadius:12,callbacks:{label:c=>` ${money(c.raw)}`}}},
          scales:{x:{grid:{display:false},border:{display:false},ticks:{color:theme.text,font:{size:10,weight:'800'}}},y:{beginAtZero:true,grid:{color:theme.grid,drawBorder:false},border:{display:false},ticks:{color:theme.muted,font:{size:9},callback:v=>money(v)}}}
        }
      });
    }

    // Theme & UI helpers
    function openModal(id) {
      document.getElementById(id).classList.remove('hidden');
      document.getElementById(id).classList.add('flex');
    }

    function closeModal(id) {
      document.getElementById(id).classList.add('hidden');
      document.getElementById(id).classList.remove('flex');
    }

    function toggleDarkMode() {
      const html = document.documentElement;
      if (html.classList.contains('dark')) {
        html.classList.remove('dark');
        document.getElementById('darkModeIcon').setAttribute('data-lucide', 'moon');
      } else {
        html.classList.add('dark');
        document.getElementById('darkModeIcon').setAttribute('data-lucide', 'sun');
      }
      lucide.createIcons();
    }

    function setThemeColor(color) {
      let root = document.documentElement;
      const banner = document.getElementById('welcomeBanner');
      const logoBg = document.getElementById('headerLogoBg');
      if (color === 'emerald') {
        root.style.setProperty('--brand-500', '#10b981');
        root.style.setProperty('--brand-600', '#059669');
        banner.className = "bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-600 to-teal-500 rounded-3xl p-6 text-white shadow-lg relative overflow-hidden flex flex-col md:flex-row justify-between items-center transition-colors duration-300";
        logoBg.className = "w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-br from-emerald-600 to-teal-400 flex items-center justify-center text-white shadow-md shadow-emerald-500/20";
      } else if (color === 'blue') {
        root.style.setProperty('--brand-500', '#3b82f6');
        root.style.setProperty('--brand-600', '#2563eb');
        banner.className = "bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-600 to-indigo-500 rounded-3xl p-6 text-white shadow-lg relative overflow-hidden flex flex-col md:flex-row justify-between items-center transition-colors duration-300";
        logoBg.className = "w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-br from-blue-600 to-indigo-400 flex items-center justify-center text-white shadow-md shadow-blue-500/20";
      } else if (color === 'purple') {
        root.style.setProperty('--brand-500', '#8b5cf6');
        root.style.setProperty('--brand-600', '#7c3aed');
        banner.className = "bg-gradient-to-r from-purple-600 to-pink-500 rounded-3xl p-6 text-white shadow-lg relative overflow-hidden flex flex-col md:flex-row justify-between items-center transition-colors duration-300";
        logoBg.className = "w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-br from-purple-600 to-pink-400 flex items-center justify-center text-white shadow-md shadow-purple-500/20";
      }
    }

    function updateWorkDayFields(){
      const st=document.getElementById('wdStatus')?.value;

      if(range) range.classList.toggle('hidden',st!=='Vacaciones');
      ['wdNormal','wd50','wd100'].forEach(id=>{const e=document.getElementById(id); if(e){e.disabled=false; e.classList.remove('opacity-50');}});
