    }
    function handleWorkDaySubmit(e){
      e.preventDefault(); const status=document.getElementById('wdStatus').value;
      const base={id:Date.now(),date:document.getElementById('wdDate').value,status,normal:parseFloat(document.getElementById('wdNormal').value)||0,extra50:parseFloat(document.getElementById('wd50').value)||0,extra100:parseFloat(document.getElementById('wd100').value)||0,details:document.getElementById('wdDetails').value};
      if(status==='Vacaciones'){
        const a=document.getElementById('wdVacationStart').value,b=document.getElementById('wdVacationEnd').value;
        if(!a||!b||b<a)return alert('Indicá correctamente desde qué día hasta qué día son las vacaciones.');
        let d=new Date(a+'T12:00:00'), end=new Date(b+'T12:00:00');
        while(d<=end){workDays.unshift({...base,id:Date.now()+Math.random(),date:d.toISOString().slice(0,10),normal:0,extra50:0,extra100:0,vacationStart:a,vacationEnd:b,details:`Vacaciones: ${a} al ${b}${base.details?' · '+base.details:''}`});d.setDate(d.getDate()+1)}
      } else workDays.unshift(base);
      saveWorkData();closeModal('workDayModal');renderWorkModule();e.target.reset();updateWorkDayFields();
    }
    async function analyzeSalaryReceiptFile(event){
      const file=event.target.files?.[0], box=document.getElementById('salaryReceiptAnalysisStatus'); if(!file)return;
      if(!file.type.startsWith('image/')) return alert('Para el análisis automático subí una imagen clara del recibo.');
      box.className='text-xs p-3 rounded-xl border bg-blue-50 border-blue-200 text-blue-700';box.classList.remove('hidden');box.textContent='Analizando imagen del recibo con OCR...';
      try{
        const {data}=await Tesseract.recognize(file,'spa+eng',{logger:m=>{if(m.status==='recognizing text')box.textContent='Analizando recibo: '+Math.round((m.progress||0)*100)+'%';}});
        const text=data.text||''; const result=parseSalaryReceiptOCR(text);
        document.getElementById('srDate').value=result.date||document.getElementById('srDate').value;
        document.getElementById('srType').value=result.type||'Mensual';
        document.getElementById('srGross').value=result.gross||'';
        document.getElementById('srDiscounts').value=result.discounts||'';
        document.getElementById('srNet').value=result.net||'';
        if(!document.getElementById('srTitle').value) document.getElementById('srTitle').value=result.title||'Recibo analizado';
        window.lastSalaryOCR={text,result}; box.className='text-xs p-3 rounded-xl border bg-emerald-50 border-emerald-200 text-emerald-700';box.innerHTML='<b>Análisis completado.</b> Se extrajeron automáticamente los datos detectados. Revisalos antes de guardar.';
      }catch(err){box.className='text-xs p-3 rounded-xl border bg-rose-50 border-rose-200 text-rose-700';box.textContent='No se pudo leer automáticamente la imagen. Podés completar los datos manualmente.';}
    }
    function parseSalaryReceiptOCR(raw){
      const text=raw.replace(/\r/g,'\n'); const lower=text.toLowerCase();
      const money=(label)=>{const i=lower.search(label); if(i<0)return 0; const part=text.slice(i,i+250); const m=part.match(/(?:\$\s*)?([0-9]{1,3}(?:[.\s][0-9]{3})*(?:,[0-9]{2})?|[0-9]+(?:[.,][0-9]{2})?)/); return m?Number(m[1].replace(/\s/g,'').replace(/\.(?=\d{3}(?:\D|$))/g,'').replace(',','.')):0};
      let gross=money(/sueldo\s*bruto|total\s*bruto|remuneraci[oó]n\s*bruta/),net=money(/sueldo\s*neto|neto\s*a\s*cobrar|total\s*neto|importe\s*neto/),discounts=money(/descuentos|total\s*descuentos/);
      if(!discounts&&gross&&net)discounts=Math.max(0,gross-net);
      const dm=text.match(/\b(\d{1,2})[\/-](\d{1,2})[\/-](\d{2,4})\b/); let date='';if(dm){let y=dm[3].length===2?'20'+dm[3]:dm[3];date=`${y}-${dm[2].padStart(2,'0')}-${dm[1].padStart(2,'0')}`}
      let type=/quincen/i.test(text)?'Quincenal':/anual/i.test(text)?'Anual':/mensual|mes\s+de/i.test(text)?'Mensual':'Mensual';
      return {gross,net,discounts,date,type,title:`Recibo ${type}${date?' '+date:''}`};
    }
    function handleSalaryReceiptSubmit(e){
      e.preventDefault();const f=document.getElementById('srFile').files[0];const r={id:Date.now(),title:document.getElementById('srTitle').value,type:document.getElementById('srType').value,date:document.getElementById('srDate').value,gross:+document.getElementById('srGross').value||0,discounts:+document.getElementById('srDiscounts').value||0,net:+document.getElementById('srNet').value||0,fileName:f?f.name:'Sin archivo',ocr:window.lastSalaryOCR?.result||null};salaryReceipts.unshift(r);if(document.getElementById('srAddAsIncome').checked)transactions.unshift({id:Date.now()+1,date:r.date,desc:r.title,category:'Sueldo/Ingreso',type:'Ingreso',amount:r.net});saveWorkData();saveData();closeModal('salaryReceiptModal');renderWorkModule();e.target.reset();window.lastSalaryOCR=null;}
    function deleteSavedModel(id){if(!confirm('¿Eliminar definitivamente este plan guardado y su archivo asociado?'))return;savedModels=savedModels.filter(m=>m.id!==id);savedModelFiles=savedModelFiles.filter(f=>f.id!==id&&f.linkedModelId!==id);saveData();renderModels();}
    const originalRenderModels=renderModels; renderModels=function(){originalRenderModels();document.querySelectorAll('#savedModelsList .text-brand-600.font-bold.hover\\:underline').forEach(()=>{});savedModels.forEach(m=>{const name=escapeHtml(m.name);const el=[...document.querySelectorAll('#savedModelsList h4')].find(x=>x.textContent===m.name);if(el&&!el.parentElement.querySelector('[data-delete-model]')){const b=document.createElement('button');b.dataset.deleteModel='1';b.className='text-xs text-rose-500 font-bold hover:underline';b.textContent='Eliminar';b.onclick=()=>deleteSavedModel(m.id);el.parentElement.querySelector('div.flex')?.appendChild(b)}})};
    async function askWorkAssistant(question){
      const chat=document.getElementById('workChatMessages'); const add=(html,side)=>{const d=document.createElement('div');d.className=side==='user'?'self-end bg-brand-600 text-white p-3 rounded-2xl rounded-tr-none text-xs max-w-md shadow-sm':'self-start bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 p-3 rounded-2xl rounded-tl-none border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 text-xs max-w-md shadow-sm';d.innerHTML=html;chat.appendChild(d);chat.scrollTop=chat.scrollHeight;return d};
      add(escapeHtml(question),'user'); const loading=add('Pensando…','ai'); const key=localStorage.getItem('plannerfi_ai_key');
      const context=`Datos laborales actuales: configuración ${JSON.stringify(workSettings)}; jornadas ${JSON.stringify(workDays.slice(0,100))}; recibos ${JSON.stringify(salaryReceipts.slice(0,30))}; notas ${JSON.stringify(workNotes.slice(0,30))}. Respondé en ${userConfig?.language==='en'?'English':userConfig?.language==='pt'?'Português':'español'}, de forma clara y útil.`;
      if(!key){loading.innerHTML='Para respuestas de IA reales y abiertas configurá una API Key en <b>Perfil → Asistente Laboral IA</b>. Sin una conexión de IA externa no es posible responder cualquier pregunta de forma generativa.';return;}
      try{const model=localStorage.getItem('plannerfi_ai_model')||'gpt-4.1-mini';const res=await fetch('https://api.openai.com/v1/responses',{method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json','Authorization':'Bearer '+key},body:JSON.stringify({model,input:[{role:'system',content:'Sos un asistente laboral y financiero. No inventes datos. '+context},{role:'user',content:question}]})});const data=await res.json();if(!res.ok)throw new Error(data.error?.message||'Error de conexión');loading.textContent=data.output_text||'No se recibió una respuesta.';}catch(err){loading.textContent='No se pudo conectar con la IA: '+err.message;}}
    function saveAISettings(){const key=document.getElementById('aiApiKeyInput').value.trim();const model=document.getElementById('aiModelInput').value;if(key)localStorage.setItem('plannerfi_ai_key',key);else localStorage.removeItem('plannerfi_ai_key');localStorage.setItem('plannerfi_ai_model',model);alert('Configuración de IA guardada en este navegador.');}
    function setThemeColor(color){const palettes={emerald:['#10b981','#059669','#047857'],blue:['#3b82f6','#2563eb','#1d4ed8'],purple:['#8b5cf6','#7c3aed','#6d28d9'],pastelPink:['#f9a8d4','#ec4899','#db2777'],pastelBlue:['#93c5fd','#60a5fa','#3b82f6'],pastelLavender:['#c4b5fd','#a78bfa','#8b5cf6'],pastelPeach:['#fdba74','#fb923c','#f97316']};const p=palettes[color]||palettes.emerald;const root=document.documentElement;root.style.setProperty('--brand-50',p[0]+'22');root.style.setProperty('--brand-100',p[0]+'44');root.style.setProperty('--brand-500',p[0]);root.style.setProperty('--brand-600',p[1]);root.style.setProperty('--brand-700',p[2]);localStorage.setItem('plannerfi_theme',color);}
    async function exportPersonalized(tabId,format='png'){const target=document.getElementById(tabId);if(!target)return;const canvas=await html2canvas(target,{scale:2,useCORS:true,backgroundColor:'#ffffff',windowWidth:target.scrollWidth,windowHeight:target.scrollHeight});const safe=(userConfig?.name||'PLANNER_FI').replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/gi,'_');if(format==='png'){const a=document.createElement('a');a.href=canvas.toDataURL('image/png');a.download=`${safe}_${tabId}_${new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)}.png`;a.click();}else{const {jsPDF}=window.jspdf;const pdf=new jsPDF('p','mm','a4');const w=190,h=canvas.height*w/canvas.width;let y=10,remain=h;const img=canvas.toDataURL('image/png');while(remain>0){pdf.addImage(img,'PNG',10,y,w,h);remain-=277;if(remain>0){pdf.addPage();y-=277;}}pdf.save(`${safe}_${tabId}_${new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)}.pdf`);}}

    const PF_PALETTES = {

      blue:{main:'#3b82f6',dark:'#2563eb',soft:'#eff6ff',soft2:'#dbeafe',accent:'#60a5fa'},

    function pfLang(){ return userConfig?.language || localStorage.getItem('plannerfi_language') || 'es'; }
    function pfCurrency(){ return userConfig?.currency || 'ARS'; }
    function pfMoney(v){
      try{return new Intl.NumberFormat(pfLang()==='pt'?'pt-BR':pfLang()==='en'?'en-US':'es-AR',{style:'currency',currency:pfCurrency(),maximumFractionDigits:2}).format(Number(v)||0)}
      catch{return '$'+Number(v||0).toLocaleString()}
    }
    function pfSafeName(){return (userConfig?.name||'PLANNER_FI').normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'').replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/gi,'_').replace(/^_|_$/g,'')||'PLANNER_FI';}
    function pfT(s){
      const dict=PF_I18N[pfLang()]||PF_I18N.es;
      return dict[s] || pfFallbackTranslate(s,pfLang());
    }

    function pfTranslateDOM(){
      const lang=pfLang();
      document.documentElement.lang=lang;
      const dynamicFinancialIds = new Set([
        'kpiIncome',
        'kpiExpenses',
        'kpiSavings',
        'sidebarBalance',
        'sidebarSavings'
      ]);

      document.querySelectorAll('body *').forEach(el=>{
        if(dynamicFinancialIds.has(el.id)) return;
        if(el.children.length===0 && el.textContent.trim()){
          if(!el.dataset.pfOriginal) el.dataset.pfOriginal=el.textContent;
          const original=el.dataset.pfOriginal;
          el.textContent=lang==='es'?original:pfT(original);
        }
      });
      document.querySelectorAll('[placeholder]').forEach(el=>{
        if(!el.dataset.pfOriginalPlaceholder) el.dataset.pfOriginalPlaceholder=el.placeholder;
        const p=el.dataset.pfOriginalPlaceholder;
        if(lang==='en') el.placeholder=p.replace('Escribí','Write').replace('Pegá','Paste').replace('consulta','question').replace('aquí','here').replace('tu API Key','your API Key');
        else if(lang==='pt') el.placeholder=p.replace('Escribí','Escreva').replace('Pegá','Cole').replace('consulta','pergunta').replace('aquí','aqui').replace('tu API Key','sua API Key');
        else el.placeholder=p;
      });
      document.querySelectorAll('[title]').forEach(el=>{
        if(!el.dataset.pfOriginalTitle) el.dataset.pfOriginalTitle=el.title;
        el.title=lang==='es'?el.dataset.pfOriginalTitle:pfT(el.dataset.pfOriginalTitle);
      });
      const title=document.querySelector('title');
      if(title) title.textContent=lang==='en'?'PLANNER.FI - Advanced Personal Finance Manager':lang==='pt'?'PLANNER.FI - Gestor Financeiro Pessoal Avançado':'PLANNER.FI - Gestor Financiero Personal Avanzado';
    }

    async function changeLanguage(lang){
      if(!['es','en','pt'].includes(lang)) lang='es';
      userConfig=userConfig||{}; userConfig.language=lang;
      localStorage.setItem('plannerfi_language',lang);
      await pfSaveUserProfile();
      renderModels(); renderTransactions(); renderDebts();
      if(!document.getElementById('tab-work')?.classList.contains('hidden')) renderWorkModule();
      if(!document.getElementById('tab-dashboard')?.classList.contains('hidden')) renderCharts();
      updateUserProfileUI();
    }

    function clearAISettings(){localStorage.removeItem('plannerfi_ai_key');document.getElementById('aiApiKeyInput').value='';alert(pfLang()==='en'?'API key removed.':pfLang()==='pt'?'Chave de API removida.':'API Key eliminada.');}
    function saveAISettings(){
      const key=(document.getElementById('aiApiKeyInput')?.value||'').trim();
      const model=(document.getElementById('aiModelInput')?.value||'gpt-4.1-mini').trim()||'gpt-4.1-mini';
      if(key)localStorage.setItem('plannerfi_ai_key',key); else localStorage.removeItem('plannerfi_ai_key');
      localStorage.setItem('plannerfi_ai_model',model);
      alert(pfLang()==='en'?'AI settings saved in this browser.':pfLang()==='pt'?'Configuração de IA salva neste navegador.':'Configuración de IA guardada en este navegador.');
    }

    async function askWorkAssistant(question){
      const chat=document.getElementById('workChatMessages'); if(!chat)return;
      const add=(html,side)=>{const d=document.createElement('div');d.className=side==='user'?'self-end bg-brand-600 text-white p-3 rounded-2xl rounded-tr-none text-xs max-w-md shadow-sm':'self-start bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 p-3 rounded-2xl rounded-tl-none border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 text-xs max-w-md shadow-sm';d.innerHTML=html;chat.appendChild(d);chat.scrollTop=chat.scrollHeight;return d;};
      add(escapeHtml(question),'user');
      const loading=add(pfLang()==='en'?'Thinking…':pfLang()==='pt'?'Pensando…':'Pensando…','ai');
      const key=localStorage.getItem('plannerfi_ai_key');
      if(!key){loading.innerHTML=pfLang()==='en'?'Configure an API key in <b>Profile → AI Work Assistant</b> to enable open-ended AI answers.':pfLang()==='pt'?'Configure uma API Key em <b>Perfil → Assistente de Trabalho IA</b> para ativar respostas abertas.':'Configurá una API Key en <b>Perfil → Asistente Laboral IA</b> para habilitar respuestas abiertas de IA.';return;}
      const totalNormal=workDays.reduce((a,d)=>a+(Number(d.normal)||0),0), total50=workDays.reduce((a,d)=>a+(Number(d.extra50)||0),0), total100=workDays.reduce((a,d)=>a+(Number(d.extra100)||0),0);
      const estimated=(totalNormal*(Number(workSettings.hourlyRate)||0))+(total50*(Number(workSettings.hourlyRate)||0)*(1+(Number(workSettings.overtime50Rate)||50)/100))+(total100*(Number(workSettings.hourlyRate)||0)*(1+(Number(workSettings.overtime100Rate)||100)/100));
      const received=salaryReceipts.reduce((a,r)=>a+(Number(r.net)||0),0);
      const context={language:pfLang(),profile:currentProfile,workSettings,summary:{workDays:workDays.length,totalNormal,total50,total100,totalHours:totalNormal+total50+total100,estimatedPay:estimated,receivedPay:received,difference:estimated-received},workDays:workDays.slice(0,120),salaryReceipts:salaryReceipts.slice(0,40),workNotes:workNotes.slice(0,40)};
      try{
        const model=localStorage.getItem('plannerfi_ai_model')||'gpt-4.1-mini';
        const res=await fetch('https://api.openai.com/v1/responses',{method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json','Authorization':'Bearer '+key},body:JSON.stringify({model,input:[{role:'system',content:'Sos el Asistente Laboral IA de PLANNER.FI. Respondé preguntas abiertas sobre trabajo, sueldo, horas, recibos, cálculos y conceptos laborales/financieros. Usá los datos proporcionados cuando corresponda, no inventes datos personales, aclarà cuando falte información y respondé en el idioma solicitado. Datos actuales: '+JSON.stringify(context)},{role:'user',content:question}]})});
        const data=await res.json(); if(!res.ok) throw new Error(data.error?.message||'Error de conexión');
        loading.textContent=data.output_text||data.output?.map(x=>x.content?.map(c=>c.text||'').join('')).join('')||'No se recibió una respuesta.';
      }catch(err){loading.textContent=(pfLang()==='en'?'Could not connect to AI: ':pfLang()==='pt'?'Não foi possível conectar à IA: ':'No se pudo conectar con la IA: ')+err.message;}
    }
    function handleWorkChatSubmit(e){e.preventDefault();const input=document.getElementById('workChatInput');const text=input?.value.trim();if(!text)return;askWorkAssistant(text);input.value='';}

    function deleteSavedModel(id){
      if(!confirm(pfLang()==='en'?'Delete this saved plan and its associated file?':pfLang()==='pt'?'Excluir este plano salvo e seu arquivo associado?':'¿Eliminar este plan guardado y su archivo asociado?'))return;
      savedModels=savedModels.filter(m=>m.id!==id);
      savedModelFiles=savedModelFiles.filter(f=>f.id!==id && f.linkedModelId!==id);
      saveData(); renderModels();
    }

    function renderModels(){
      const list=document.getElementById('savedModelsList'); if(!list)return;
      list.innerHTML='';
      if(!savedModels.length && !savedModelFiles.length){list.innerHTML=`<p class="text-slate-400 text-sm">${pfLang()==='en'?'No saved plans or files yet.':pfLang()==='pt'?'Ainda não há planos ou arquivos salvos.':'No hay planes ni archivos guardados todavía.'}</p>`;pfTranslateDOM();return;}
      if(savedModels.length){
        list.innerHTML+=`<h4 class="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400">${pfT('Planes guardados')}</h4>`;
        savedModels.forEach(m=>{
          list.innerHTML+=`<div class="p-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-700/30 space-y-2">
            <div class="flex justify-between items-center gap-3"><h4 class="font-bold text-sm text-brand-600">${escapeHtml(m.name)}</h4><div class="flex gap-3 items-center"><span class="text-xs text-slate-400">${escapeHtml(m.date)}</span><button onclick="renameSavedModel(${m.id})" class="text-xs text-slate-500 font-bold hover:underline">${pfT('Renombrar')}</button><button onclick="downloadPlanModel(${m.id})" class="text-xs text-brand-600 font-bold hover:underline">${pfT('Descargar')}</button><button data-delete-model="1" onclick="deleteSavedModel(${m.id})" class="text-xs text-rose-500 font-bold hover:underline">${pfT('Eliminar')}</button></div></div>
            <div class="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 text-xs bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 p-3 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700"><div>${pfT('Perfil:')} <b>${escapeHtml(m.profile)}</b></div><div>${pfLang()==='en'?'Transactions':pfLang()==='pt'?'Movimentos':'Movimientos'}: <b>${m.transCount}</b></div><div>${pfT('Diferencia')}: <b class="text-emerald-500">${pfMoney(m.balance||0)}</b></div><div class="col-span-3 text-slate-400">${pfLang()==='en'?'Type':pfLang()==='pt'?'Tipo':'Tipo'}: <b class="text-brand-600">${escapeHtml(m.type||'Plan financiero')}</b>${m.period?' · '+(pfLang()==='en'?'Period':pfLang()==='pt'?'Período':'Período')+': <b>'+escapeHtml(m.period)+'</b>':''}</div></div>
          </div>`;
        });
      }
      if(savedModelFiles.length){
        list.innerHTML+=`<h4 class="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400 pt-3">${pfT('Archivos guardados')}</h4>`;
        savedModelFiles.forEach(f=>{list.innerHTML+=`<div class="p-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 flex items-center justify-between gap-4"><div class="flex items-center gap-3 min-w-0"><div class="p-2 rounded-lg bg-brand-50 text-brand-600"><i data-lucide="file" class="w-5 h-5"></i></div><div class="min-w-0"><h4 class="font-bold text-sm truncate">${escapeHtml(f.displayName||f.name)}</h4><p class="text-xs text-slate-400">${escapeHtml(f.type||'Archivo')} · ${formatBytes(f.size)} · ${escapeHtml(f.date)}</p></div></div><div class="flex gap-2 shrink-0"><button onclick="downloadModelFile(${f.id})" class="text-xs bg-brand-600 text-white px-3 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold">${pfT('Ver / Descargar')}</button><button onclick="deleteModelFile(${f.id})" class="text-xs text-rose-500 font-bold px-2">${pfT('Eliminar')}</button></div></div>`});
      }
      lucide.createIcons();
    }

    function downloadModelFile(id){
      const f=savedModelFiles.find(x=>x.id===id); if(!f)return;
      // Los artefactos generados por PLANNER.FI se descargan como PDF visual, nunca como JSON.
      if(f.linkedModelId){
        const m=savedModels.find(x=>x.id===f.linkedModelId);
        if(m){window.__pfExportSnapshot=m.snapshot||{};exportReport((m.type||'').toLowerCase().includes('trabajo')?'work':'dashboard','pdf',m.name).finally(()=>{window.__pfExportSnapshot=null;});return;}
      }
      const a=document.createElement('a');a.href=f.data;a.download=f.name||'archivo';a.click();
    }

    /* Editor real de Modelos / Planes y Archivos.
       Permite actualizar una copia guardada sin alterar los módulos originales. */
    let pfModelsEditorMode = null;

    function pfEditorClose(){
      const modal=document.getElementById('pfModelsEditorModal');
      if(modal) modal.remove();
      pfModelsEditorMode=null;
      document.body.style.overflow='';
    }

    function pfEditorField(label, id, value='', type='text', extra=''){
      return `<label class="pf-editor-field"><span>${label}</span><input id="${id}" type="${type}" value="${escapeHtml(value)}" ${extra}></label>`;
    }

    function pfOpenModelsEditor(title, bodyHtml, onSave){
      pfEditorClose();
      const modal=document.createElement('div');
      modal.id='pfModelsEditorModal';
      modal.className='pf-model-editor-overlay';
      modal.innerHTML=`<div class="pf-model-editor" role="dialog" aria-modal="true">
        <div class="pf-model-editor-head">
          <div><span>EDITAR BIBLIOTECA</span><h3>${escapeHtml(title)}</h3><p>Los cambios se guardan solamente en este elemento guardado.</p></div>
          <button type="button" onclick="pfEditorClose()" aria-label="Cerrar"><i data-lucide="x"></i></button>
        </div>
        <form id="pfModelsEditorForm">
          <div class="pf-model-editor-body">${bodyHtml}</div>
          <div class="pf-model-editor-foot"><button type="button" class="pf-editor-cancel" onclick="pfEditorClose()">Cancelar</button><button type="submit" class="pf-editor-save"><i data-lucide="save"></i> Guardar cambios</button></div>
        </form>
      </div>`;
      modal.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target===modal)pfEditorClose();});
      document.body.appendChild(modal);
      document.body.style.overflow='hidden';
      lucide.createIcons();
      document.getElementById('pfModelsEditorForm').addEventListener('submit',e=>{
        e.preventDefault();
        try{ onSave(); }catch(err){console.error(err);alert('No se pudieron guardar los cambios.');}
      });
    }

    function pfEditorReceiptRow(receipt,index){
      const r=receipt||{};
      return `<div class="pf-receipt-row" data-receipt-row>
        <div class="pf-receipt-row-head"><b>Recibo ${index+1}</b><button type="button" onclick="this.closest('[data-receipt-row]').remove()">Quitar</button></div>
        <div class="pf-editor-grid">
          ${pfEditorField('Título','pfRecTitle'+index,r.title||'', 'text')}
          ${pfEditorField('Fecha','pfRecDate'+index,r.date||'', 'date')}
          ${pfEditorField('Tipo','pfRecType'+index,r.type||'Mensual', 'text')}
          ${pfEditorField('Bruto ($)','pfRecGross'+index,r.gross??'', 'number','step="0.01"')}
          ${pfEditorField('Descuentos ($)','pfRecDiscounts'+index,r.discounts??'', 'number','step="0.01"')}
          ${pfEditorField('Neto ($)','pfRecNet'+index,r.net??'', 'number','step="0.01"')}
        </div>
      </div>`;
    }

    function pfAddEditorReceipt(){
      const box=document.getElementById('pfEditorReceipts');
      if(!box)return;
      const index=box.querySelectorAll('[data-receipt-row]').length;
      box.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',pfEditorReceiptRow({},index));
    }

    function pfEditSavedPlan(id){
      const m=savedModels.find(x=>x.id===id);
      if(!m)return;
      const snap=m.snapshot||{};
      const receipts=Array.isArray(snap.salaryReceipts)?snap.salaryReceipts:[];
      const manualInfo=m.manualInfo||snap.manualInfo||'';
      const body=`
        ${pfEditorField('Nombre del plan','pfEditPlanName',m.name||'')}
        <div class="pf-editor-grid">
          ${pfEditorField('Período','pfEditPlanPeriod',m.period||'', 'text')}
          ${pfEditorField('Tipo','pfEditPlanType',m.type||'Plan financiero', 'text')}
        </div>
        <label class="pf-editor-field"><span>Información / anotaciones manuales</span><textarea id="pfEditPlanInfo" rows="5" placeholder="Agregá información que quieras conservar dentro de este historial...">${escapeHtml(manualInfo)}</textarea></label>
        <div class="pf-editor-section"><div class="pf-editor-section-head"><div><b>Recibos de sueldo</b><small>Podés agregar los recibos que recibas en los próximos meses.</small></div><button type="button" class="pf-editor-add" onclick="pfAddEditorReceipt()"><i data-lucide="plus"></i> Agregar recibo</button></div><div id="pfEditorReceipts">${receipts.map(pfEditorReceiptRow).join('')}</div></div>
        <div class="pf-editor-hint"><i data-lucide="info"></i><span>Este editor modifica la copia guardada en Modelos / Planes. Tus datos actuales de Trabajo y Sueldo no se modifican.</span></div>`;
      pfOpenModelsEditor(m.name||'Plan guardado',body,()=>{
        const name=document.getElementById('pfEditPlanName').value.trim();
        if(!name){alert('El plan necesita un nombre.');return;}
        m.name=name;
        m.period=document.getElementById('pfEditPlanPeriod').value.trim();
        m.type=document.getElementById('pfEditPlanType').value.trim()||m.type;
        m.manualInfo=document.getElementById('pfEditPlanInfo').value.trim();
        if(!m.snapshot)m.snapshot={};
        const rows=[...document.querySelectorAll('#pfEditorReceipts [data-receipt-row]')];
        m.snapshot.salaryReceipts=rows.map((row,i)=>({
          id: row.dataset.id || ('edited-'+Date.now()+'-'+i),
          title: row.querySelector('#pfRecTitle'+i)?.value.trim()||'Recibo',
          date: row.querySelector('#pfRecDate'+i)?.value||'',
          type: row.querySelector('#pfRecType'+i)?.value.trim()||'Mensual',
          gross: Number(row.querySelector('#pfRecGross'+i)?.value)||0,
          discounts: Number(row.querySelector('#pfRecDiscounts'+i)?.value)||0,
          net: Number(row.querySelector('#pfRecNet'+i)?.value)||0,
          fileName: row.querySelector('#pfRecTitle'+i)?.value.trim()||'Recibo'
        }));
        m.snapshot.manualInfo=m.manualInfo;
        m.updatedAt=new Date().toLocaleString();
        const linked=savedModelFiles.find(f=>f.linkedModelId===id);
        if(linked){linked.displayName=m.name;linked.name=m.name+'.json';linked.date=m.updatedAt;}
        // Mantener el resumen coherente cuando el plan es laboral.
        if(Array.isArray(m.snapshot.salaryReceipts)){
          m.snapshot.analysis=m.snapshot.analysis||{};
          m.snapshot.analysis.receivedPay=m.snapshot.salaryReceipts.reduce((a,r)=>a+(Number(r.net)||0),0);
          if(Array.isArray(m.snapshot.workDays)){
            const normal=m.snapshot.workDays.reduce((a,d)=>a+(Number(d.normal)||0),0);
            const e50=m.snapshot.workDays.reduce((a,d)=>a+(Number(d.extra50)||0),0);
            const e100=m.snapshot.workDays.reduce((a,d)=>a+(Number(d.extra100)||0),0);
            const rate=Number(m.snapshot.workSettings?.hourlyRate)||0;
            const est=normal*rate+e50*rate*(1+(Number(m.snapshot.workSettings?.overtime50Rate)||50)/100)+e100*rate*(1+(Number(m.snapshot.workSettings?.overtime100Rate)||100)/100);
            m.snapshot.analysis.totalNormal=normal;m.snapshot.analysis.total50=e50;m.snapshot.analysis.total100=e100;
            m.snapshot.analysis.totalHours=normal+e50+e100;m.snapshot.analysis.estimatedPay=est;
            m.snapshot.analysis.difference=est-m.snapshot.analysis.receivedPay;
            m.balance=est-m.snapshot.analysis.receivedPay;
          }
        }
        // Regenerar el payload del archivo vinculado para que Descargar/Visualizar usen la versión editada.
        if(linked){
          const payload={name:m.name,type:m.type,period:m.period||null,profile:m.profile,date:m.date,snapshot:m.snapshot||{}};
          const json=JSON.stringify(payload,null,2);
          linked.data='data:application/json;charset=utf-8,'+encodeURIComponent(json);
          linked.size=new Blob([json]).size;
        }
        saveData();pfEditorClose();renderModels();
      });
    }

    function pfEditSavedFile(id){
      const f=savedModelFiles.find(x=>x.id===id);
      if(!f)return;
      const linked=f.linkedModelId?savedModels.find(m=>m.id===f.linkedModelId):null;
      if(linked){pfEditSavedPlan(linked.id);return;}
      const info=f.manualInfo||'';
      const body=`
        ${pfEditorField('Nombre del archivo','pfEditFileName',f.displayName||f.name||'')}
        <label class="pf-editor-field"><span>Información / anotaciones</span><textarea id="pfEditFileInfo" rows="7" placeholder="Agregá contexto, notas, referencia del documento, período, cambios, etc.">${escapeHtml(info)}</textarea></label>
        <div class="pf-editor-hint"><i data-lucide="info"></i><span>El archivo original no se modifica. Se actualizan su nombre y la información asociada dentro de tu biblioteca.</span></div>`;
      pfOpenModelsEditor(f.displayName||f.name||'Archivo guardado',body,()=>{
        const name=document.getElementById('pfEditFileName').value.trim();
        if(!name){alert('El archivo necesita un nombre.');return;}
        f.displayName=name;
        f.manualInfo=document.getElementById('pfEditFileInfo').value.trim();
        f.updatedAt=new Date().toLocaleString();
        saveData();pfEditorClose();renderModels();
      });
    }
    function downloadPlanModel(id){
      const m=savedModels.find(x=>x.id===id); if(!m)return;
      // Los planes ya no se descargan como JSON. Se exportan como informe visual PDF.
      const type=(m.type||'').toLowerCase().includes('trabajo')?'work':'dashboard';
      window.__pfExportSnapshot=m.snapshot||{};
      exportReport(type,'pdf',m.name).finally(()=>{window.__pfExportSnapshot=null;});
    }

    function pfBuildDashboardReport(snapshot){
      const tx=snapshot.transactions||transactions;
      const incomes=tx.filter(t=>t.type==='Ingreso').reduce((a,t)=>a+(Number(t.amount)||0),0);
      const expenses=tx.filter(t=>t.type==='Gasto').reduce((a,t)=>a+(Number(t.amount)||0),0);
      const savings=Math.max(0,incomes-expenses), balance=incomes-expenses;
      const cats=['Alimentación','Vivienda','Transporte','Ocio','Salud','Suscripciones','Educación'];
      const catRows=cats.map(c=>({name:c,value:tx.filter(t=>t.type==='Gasto'&&t.category===c).reduce((a,t)=>a+(Number(t.amount)||0),0)})).filter(x=>x.value>0).sort((a,b)=>b.value-a.value);
      const maxCat=Math.max(...catRows.map(x=>x.value),1);
      const totalCat=catRows.reduce((a,x)=>a+x.value,0)||1;
      const recent=tx.slice(0,8);
      const incomeRows=recent.filter(t=>t.type==='Ingreso');
      const expenseRows=recent.filter(t=>t.type==='Gasto');
      const incomeTable=incomeRows.length?incomeRows.map(t=>`<tr><td>${escapeHtml(t.date||'-')}</td><td>${escapeHtml(t.desc||'-')}</td><td>${escapeHtml(t.category||'-')}</td><td class="num">${pfMoney(t.amount)}</td></tr>`).join(''):`<tr><td colspan="4" class="empty">Sin ingresos registrados.</td></tr>`;
      const expenseTable=expenseRows.length?expenseRows.map(t=>`<tr><td>${escapeHtml(t.date||'-')}</td><td>${escapeHtml(t.desc||'-')}</td><td>${escapeHtml(t.category||'-')}</td><td class="num">${pfMoney(t.amount)}</td></tr>`).join(''):`<tr><td colspan="4" class="empty">Sin gastos registrados.</td></tr>`;
      const allRows=recent.length?recent.map(t=>`<tr><td>${escapeHtml(t.date||'-')}</td><td>${escapeHtml(t.desc||'-')}</td><td>${escapeHtml(t.category||'-')}</td><td>${escapeHtml(t.type||'-')}</td><td class="num">${pfMoney(t.amount)}</td></tr>`).join(''):`<tr><td colspan="5" class="empty">No hay movimientos registrados.</td></tr>`;
      const categoryRows=catRows.length?catRows.map(x=>`<tr><td>${escapeHtml(x.name)}</td><td class="num">${pfMoney(x.value)}</td><td><div class="sheet-progress"><i style="width:${Math.max(6,Math.round(x.value/maxCat*100))}%"></i></div></td><td class="num">${Math.round(x.value/totalCat*100)}%</td></tr>`).join(''):`<tr><td colspan="4" class="empty">Todavía no hay gastos por categoría.</td></tr>`;
      const incomePct=incomes/(Math.max(incomes,expenses)||1)*100;
      const expensePct=expenses/(Math.max(incomes,expenses)||1)*100;
      const savingGoal=Math.max(incomes*.20,1), savingPct=Math.min(100,Math.round(savings/savingGoal*100));
      const recommendation=balance<0?'Tus gastos superan tus ingresos. Priorizá revisar las categorías con mayor consumo y definí un límite para el próximo período.':expenses>incomes*.8?'Tus gastos representan una parte alta de tus ingresos. Revisá gastos variables y suscripciones antes de aumentar compromisos.':'Tu balance es saludable. Podés separar una parte del excedente para ahorro, emergencia u objetivos.';
      const topCategory=catRows[0]?.name||'Sin categoría';
      return `<div class="report-page report-dashboard report-sheet">
        <header class="sheet-header"><div><div class="sheet-brand">PLANNER.FI</div><h1>PERSONAL BUDGET PLANNER</h1><h2>RESUMEN FINANCIERO PERSONAL</h2><p>Planificá, registrá y analizá tu dinero en una sola vista.</p></div><div class="sheet-date"><b>INFORME</b><span>${new Date().toLocaleDateString()}</span><small>${escapeHtml(userConfig?.name||'Usuario')}</small></div></header>
        <section class="sheet-overview"><div class="sheet-section-title"><b>1</b><div><h3>OVERVIEW DASHBOARD</h3><p>Una vista rápida de tu situación financiera actual.</p></div></div><div class="sheet-kpis"><div><span>INGRESOS TOTALES</span><b>${pfMoney(incomes)}</b></div><div><span>GASTOS TOTALES</span><b>${pfMoney(expenses)}</b></div><div><span>AHORRO ACTUAL</span><b>${pfMoney(savings)}</b></div><div><span>BALANCE</span><b>${pfMoney(balance)}</b></div></div><div class="sheet-overview-grid"><article><h4>INGRESOS vs GASTOS</h4><div class="sheet-bars"><div><i style="height:${Math.max(10,Math.round(incomePct*1.15))}px"></i><span>Ingresos</span><b>${pfMoney(incomes)}</b></div><div><i class="expense" style="height:${Math.max(10,Math.round(expensePct*1.15))}px"></i><span>Gastos</span><b>${pfMoney(expenses)}</b></div></div></article><article><h4>GASTOS POR CATEGORÍA</h4><div class="sheet-category-mini">${catRows.slice(0,5).map(x=>`<div><span>${escapeHtml(x.name)}</span><em><i style="width:${Math.max(5,Math.round(x.value/maxCat*100))}%"></i></em><b>${Math.round(x.value/totalCat*100)}%</b></div>`).join('')||'<p class="empty">Sin datos todavía.</p>'}</div></article><article><h4>PROGRESO DE AHORRO</h4><div class="sheet-saving"><div class="sheet-ring" style="--sheet-pct:${savingPct}"><span>${savingPct}%</span></div><b>${pfMoney(savings)}</b><small>Referencia: 20% de tus ingresos</small></div></article></div></section>
        <div class="sheet-two-col"><section class="sheet-panel"><div class="sheet-panel-head"><h3>INGRESOS</h3><span>Registro reciente</span></div><table><thead><tr><th>FECHA</th><th>DESCRIPCIÓN</th><th>CATEGORÍA</th><th class="num">MONTO</th></tr></thead><tbody>${incomeTable}</tbody></table><div class="sheet-total">TOTAL INGRESOS <b>${pfMoney(incomes)}</b></div></section><section class="sheet-panel"><div class="sheet-panel-head"><h3>GASTOS</h3><span>Registro reciente</span></div><table><thead><tr><th>FECHA</th><th>DESCRIPCIÓN</th><th>CATEGORÍA</th><th class="num">MONTO</th></tr></thead><tbody>${expenseTable}</tbody></table><div class="sheet-total">TOTAL GASTOS <b>${pfMoney(expenses)}</b></div></section></div>
        <div class="sheet-two-col"><section class="sheet-panel"><div class="sheet-panel-head"><h3>PRESUPUESTO POR CATEGORÍA</h3><span>Distribución de gastos</span></div><table><thead><tr><th>CATEGORÍA</th><th class="num">GASTADO</th><th>PROGRESO</th><th class="num">%</th></tr></thead><tbody>${categoryRows}</tbody></table><div class="sheet-total">CATEGORÍA PRINCIPAL <b>${escapeHtml(topCategory)}</b></div></section><section class="sheet-panel"><div class="sheet-panel-head"><h3>RESUMEN DEL PERÍODO</h3><span>Estadísticas principales</span></div><div class="sheet-stats"><div><span>MOVIMIENTOS</span><b>${tx.length}</b></div><div><span>INGRESOS</span><b>${incomeRows.length}</b></div><div><span>GASTOS</span><b>${expenseRows.length}</b></div><div><span>BALANCE</span><b>${pfMoney(balance)}</b></div></div><div class="sheet-advice"><h4>RECOMENDACIÓN PERSONALIZADA</h4><p>${recommendation}</p></div><div class="sheet-checks"><span>✓ Datos calculados automáticamente</span><span>✓ Análisis según tus movimientos</span><span>✓ Preparado para seguimiento mensual</span></div></section></div>
        <section class="sheet-panel sheet-movements"><div class="sheet-panel-head"><h3>MOVIMIENTOS RECIENTES</h3><span>Detalle consolidado</span></div><table><thead><tr><th>FECHA</th><th>DESCRIPCIÓN</th><th>CATEGORÍA</th><th>TIPO</th><th class="num">MONTO</th></tr></thead><tbody>${allRows}</tbody></table></section>
        <footer class="sheet-footer">PLANNER.FI · CONTROLÁ TU DINERO. ORGANIZÁ TU FUTURO. · ${new Date().toLocaleDateString()}</footer>
      </div>`;
    }

    function pfBuildWorkReport(snapshot){
      const ws=snapshot.workSettings||workSettings, days=snapshot.workDays||workDays, receipts=snapshot.salaryReceipts||salaryReceipts;
      const normal=days.reduce((a,d)=>a+(Number(d.normal)||0),0), e50=days.reduce((a,d)=>a+(Number(d.extra50)||0),0), e100=days.reduce((a,d)=>a+(Number(d.extra100)||0),0);
      const rate=Number(ws.hourlyRate)||0, r50=rate*(1+(Number(ws.overtime50Rate)||50)/100), r100=rate*(1+(Number(ws.overtime100Rate)||100)/100);
      const estimated=normal*rate+e50*r50+e100*r100, received=receipts.reduce((a,r)=>a+(Number(r.net)||0),0), diff=estimated-received;
      const dayRows=days.slice(0,18).map(d=>`<tr><td>${escapeHtml(d.date||'-')}</td><td>${escapeHtml(d.status||'-')}</td><td>${Number(d.normal)||0}h</td><td>${Number(d.extra50)||0}h</td><td>${Number(d.extra100)||0}h</td><td>${pfMoney((Number(d.normal)||0)*rate+(Number(d.extra50)||0)*r50+(Number(d.extra100)||0)*r100)}</td></tr>`).join('')||`<tr><td colspan="6" class="empty">${pfLang()==='en'?'No workdays.':pfLang()==='pt'?'Sem jornadas.':'No hay jornadas.'}</td></tr>`;
      const receiptRows=receipts.slice(0,10).map(r=>`<tr><td>${escapeHtml(r.title||'-')}</td><td>${escapeHtml(r.type||'-')}</td><td>${pfMoney(r.gross)}</td><td>${pfMoney(r.discounts)}</td><td><b>${pfMoney(r.net)}</b></td></tr>`).join('')||`<tr><td colspan="5" class="empty">${pfLang()==='en'?'No payslips.':pfLang()==='pt'?'Sem contracheques.':'No hay recibos.'}</td></tr>`;
      return `<div class="report-page report-work"><div class="report-top work-top"><div><div class="report-logo">PLANNER.FI · WORK</div><h1>${pfLang()==='en'?'Work & Salary Report':pfLang()==='pt'?'Relatório de Trabalho e Salário':'Informe de Trabajo y Sueldo'}</h1><p>${escapeHtml(ws.companyName||'')} · ${escapeHtml(ws.jobTitle||'')}</p></div><div class="report-date"><b>${pfLang()==='en'?'Report date':pfLang()==='pt'?'Data do relatório':'Fecha del informe'}</b><br>${new Date().toLocaleDateString()}</div></div>
      <div class="work-kpis"><div><span>${pfLang()==='en'?'Hours Worked':pfLang()==='pt'?'Horas Trabalhadas':'Horas Trabajadas'}</span><b>${normal+e50+e100} h</b></div><div><span>${pfLang()==='en'?'Overtime 50%':pfLang()==='pt'?'Horas 50%':'Horas 50%'}</span><b>${e50} h</b></div><div><span>${pfLang()==='en'?'Overtime 100%':pfLang()==='pt'?'Horas 100%':'Horas 100%'}</span><b>${e100} h</b></div><div><span>${pfLang()==='en'?'Estimated Pay':pfLang()==='pt'?'Pagamento Estimado':'Pago Estimado'}</span><b>${pfMoney(estimated)}</b></div><div><span>${pfLang()==='en'?'Received Pay':pfLang()==='pt'?'Pagamento Recebido':'Pago Recibido'}</span><b>${pfMoney(received)}</b></div><div><span>${pfLang()==='en'?'Difference':pfLang()==='pt'?'Diferença':'Diferencia'}</span><b>${pfMoney(diff)}</b></div></div>
      <section class="report-section teal"><h2>${pfLang()==='en'?'WORKDAYS & OVERTIME':pfLang()==='pt'?'JORNADAS E HORAS EXTRAS':'JORNADAS Y HORAS EXTRAS'}</h2><table><thead><tr><th>${pfT('Fecha')}</th><th>${pfLang()==='en'?'Status':pfLang()==='pt'?'Estado':'Estado'}</th><th>Norm.</th><th>50%</th><th>100%</th><th>${pfLang()==='en'?'Estimated Pay':pfLang()==='pt'?'Pagamento Estimado':'Pago Est.'}</th></tr></thead><tbody>${dayRows}</tbody></table></section>
      <section class="report-section blue"><h2>${pfLang()==='en'?'PAYSLIPS COMPARISON':pfLang()==='pt'?'COMPARAÇÃO DE CONTRACHEQUES':'COMPARACIÓN DE RECIBOS'}</h2><table><thead><tr><th>${pfLang()==='en'?'Period / Title':pfLang()==='pt'?'Período / Título':'Período / Título'}</th><th>${pfT('Tipo')}</th><th>${pfLang()==='en'?'Gross':pfLang()==='pt'?'Bruto':'Bruto'}</th><th>${pfLang()==='en'?'Discounts':pfLang()==='pt'?'Descontos':'Descuentos'}</th><th>${pfLang()==='en'?'Net':pfLang()==='pt'?'Líquido':'Neto'}</th></tr></thead><tbody>${receiptRows}</tbody></table></section>
      <div class="report-work-analysis"><div><b>${pfLang()==='en'?'Hourly rate':'Valor por hora'}:</b> ${pfMoney(rate)}</div><div><b>${pfLang()==='en'?'50% overtime rate':'Valor hora 50%'}:</b> ${pfMoney(r50)}</div><div><b>${pfLang()==='en'?'100% overtime rate':'Valor hora 100%'}:</b> ${pfMoney(r100)}</div><div><b>${pfLang()==='en'?'Company':'Empresa'}:</b> ${escapeHtml(ws.companyName||'-')}</div></div>
      <div class="report-footer">PLANNER.FI · ${pfLang()==='en'?'Work & salary report':pfLang()==='pt'?'Relatório de trabalho e salário':'Informe de trabajo y sueldo'} · ${new Date().toLocaleDateString()}</div></div>`;
    }

    async function exportReport(kind,format='png',customName=''){
      if(typeof html2canvas==='undefined'){alert('html2canvas no está disponible.');return;}
      const snapshot=window.__pfExportSnapshot|| (kind==='dashboard'?{transactions:[...transactions]}:{workSettings:{...workSettings},workDays:[...workDays],salaryReceipts:[...salaryReceipts]});
      const holder=document.createElement('div'); holder.id='pf-export-stage'; holder.innerHTML=kind==='dashboard'?pfBuildDashboardReport(snapshot):pfBuildWorkReport(snapshot); document.body.appendChild(holder);
      const target=holder.firstElementChild;
      try{
        const canvas=await html2canvas(target,{scale:2,backgroundColor:'#ffffff',useCORS:true,logging:false,windowWidth:target.scrollWidth,windowHeight:target.scrollHeight});
        const name=(customName|| (kind==='dashboard'?'Dashboard':'Trabajo_y_Sueldo')).normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'').replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/gi,'_');
        const date=new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10);
        if(format==='png'){const a=document.createElement('a');a.href=canvas.toDataURL('image/png');a.download=`${pfSafeName()}_${name}_${date}.png`;a.click();}
        else {const {jsPDF}=window.jspdf||{};if(!jsPDF)throw new Error('jsPDF no está disponible.');const pdf=new jsPDF('p','mm','a4');const img=canvas.toDataURL('image/png');const pageW=190;const pageH=277;const imgH=canvas.height*pageW/canvas.width;let offset=0;while(offset<imgH){pdf.addImage(img,'PNG',10,10-offset,pageW,imgH);offset+=pageH;if(offset<imgH)pdf.addPage();}pdf.save(`${pfSafeName()}_${name}_${date}.pdf`);}
      }finally{holder.remove();}
    }

    function setThemeColor(color){
      const p=PF_PALETTES[color]||PF_PALETTES.emerald, root=document.documentElement;
      root.dataset.palette=color;
      root.style.setProperty('--brand-50',p.soft);root.style.setProperty('--brand-100',p.soft2);root.style.setProperty('--brand-500',p.main);root.style.setProperty('--brand-600',p.dark);root.style.setProperty('--brand-700',p.dark);root.style.setProperty('--pf-accent',p.accent);root.style.setProperty('--pf-soft',p.soft);root.style.setProperty('--pf-soft2',p.soft2);
      localStorage.setItem('plannerfi_theme',color);
      const banner=document.getElementById('welcomeBanner'), logo=document.getElementById('headerLogoBg');
      if(banner){banner.style.background=`linear-gradient(135deg, ${p.dark}, ${p.main})`;}
      if(logo){logo.style.background=`linear-gradient(135deg, ${p.dark}, ${p.accent})`;}
      pfApplyPaletteClasses();
      if(pieChartInstance||barChartInstance)renderCharts(); if(workHoursPieChartInstance||salaryEvolutionChartInstance)renderWorkCharts();
    }
    function pfApplyPaletteClasses(){
      const r=document.documentElement;
      r.style.setProperty('--pf-accent',getComputedStyle(r).getPropertyValue('--brand-500').trim()||'#10b981');
    }
    function pfLoadTheme(){setThemeColor(localStorage.getItem('plannerfi_theme')||'emerald');}

    const _saveArtifactInModels=saveArtifactInModels;
    saveArtifactInModels=function(model){
      const ok=_saveArtifactInModels(model);
      if(!ok)return false;
      // El JSON queda solo como estructura interna local; el botón Descargar genera PDF visual.
      const linked=savedModelFiles.find(f=>f.linkedModelId===model.id||f.id===model.id);
      if(linked){linked.sourceType=model.type;linked.type='application/pdf';linked.name=`${model.name}.pdf`;}
      saveData();renderModels();return true;
    };
